    const uint32_t nblk = (count + slots - 1) / slots;   // every warp of the block walks the same number of rounds (the syncs)
    for (uint32_t it = 0; it < nblk; ++it) {
        const uint32_t gi = slot + it * slots;
        const bool live = gi < count;
        const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
        uint32_t S[8], M[8], F[8];
        // S = BLAKE3(prefix || u64be(N)), every warp
        {
            const uint32_t nhi = bswap32((uint32_t)(nonce >> 32)), nlo = bswap32((uint32_t)nonce);
            b3_words_xof<104>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 24 ? pw[i] : (i == 24 ? nhi : nlo); }, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
        }
        unsigned int R[56];
        // the init: registers 56 bk .. 56 bk + 55 = XOF words 56 bk .., blocks (56 bk) / 16 .. (56 bk + 55) / 16 (four a bank)
#if IH_R17_TAILS
#define IH_R17_INIT(BK) { b3_words_xof<84>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 5 ? tiw[i] : i < 13 ? Kw[i - 5] : S[i - 13]; }, o); uint32_t x[8]; \
            _Pragma("unroll") for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) x[k] = o[k]; \
            _Pragma("unroll") for (uint32_t r = 0; r < 224u; ++r) { x[r & 7u] = ih_r17q_fold(x[r & 7u], r); if (r >= 56u * (BK) && r < 56u * (BK) + 56u) R[r - 56u * (BK)] = x[r & 7u]; } }
#else
#define IH_R17_INIT(BK) { _Pragma("unroll") for (uint32_t t = (56u * (BK)) / 16u; t <= (56u * (BK) + 55u) / 16u; ++t) { \
            b3_words_xof<84>(t, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 5 ? tiw[i] : i < 13 ? Kw[i - 5] : S[i - 13]; }, o); \
            _Pragma("unroll") for (uint32_t k = 0; k < 16u; ++k) { const uint32_t w = 16u * t + k; if (w >= 56u * (BK) && w < 56u * (BK) + 56u) R[w - 56u * (BK)] = o[k]; } } }
#endif
        if (bk == 0) { IH_R17_INIT(0); ih_r16_prog_b0(R); }
        else if (bk == 1) { IH_R17_INIT(1); ih_r16_prog_b1(R); }
        else if (bk == 2) { IH_R17_INIT(2); ih_r16_prog_b2(R); }
        else { IH_R17_INIT(3); ih_r16_prog_b3(R); }
#undef IH_R17_INIT
        uint32_t cv[8];
#if IH_R17_TAILS
        // revision 3's mix: the eight fold chains over the 224 registers in order, bank by bank through the warps (the chain state x[8] handed forward)
        if (bk == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = Kw[i];
            _Pragma("unroll") for (uint32_t r = 0; r < 56u; ++r) cv[r & 7u] = ih_r17q_fold(cv[r & 7u], R[r]);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h01[lane][i] = cv[i];
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (bk == 1) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = h01[lane][i];
            _Pragma("unroll") for (uint32_t r = 0; r < 56u; ++r) cv[r & 7u] = ih_r17q_fold(cv[r & 7u], R[r]);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h12[lane][i] = cv[i];
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (bk == 2) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = h12[lane][i];
            _Pragma("unroll") for (uint32_t r = 0; r < 56u; ++r) cv[r & 7u] = ih_r17q_fold(cv[r & 7u], R[r]);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h23[lane][i] = cv[i];
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (bk == 3) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = h23[lane][i];
            _Pragma("unroll") for (uint32_t r = 0; r < 56u; ++r) cv[r & 7u] = ih_r17q_fold(cv[r & 7u], R[r]);
            b3_words_xof<83>(0, [&](uint32_t i) {
                if (i < 4) return tmw[i];
                if (i == 4) return tmw[4] | (Kw[0] << 24);
                if (i < 12) return (Kw[i - 5] >> 8) | (Kw[i - 4] << 24);
                if (i == 12) return (Kw[7] >> 8) | (cv[0] << 24);
                if (i < 20) return (cv[i - 13] >> 8) | (cv[i - 12] << 24);
                return cv[7] >> 8;
            }, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
#else
        // the mix chain: bank 0 blocks 0..3, hand cv and R[51..55]
        if (bk == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
            ih_r17_mix_blocks<0, 0, 0, 0, 3>(R, nullptr, Kw, tmw, cv, o);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h01[lane][i] = cv[i];
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) h01[lane][8 + i] = R[51 + i];
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (bk == 1) {
            uint32_t B[5];
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = h01[lane][i];
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) B[i] = h01[lane][8 + i];
            ih_r17_mix_blocks<1, 51, 5, 4, 6>(R, B, Kw, tmw, cv, o);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h12[lane][i] = cv[i];
            for (int i = 0; i < 13; ++i) h12[lane][8 + i] = R[43 + i];   // R[99..111]
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (bk == 2) {
            uint32_t B[13];
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = h12[lane][i];
            for (int i = 0; i < 13; ++i) B[i] = h12[lane][8 + i];
            ih_r17_mix_blocks<2, 99, 13, 7, 10>(R, B, Kw, tmw, cv, o);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h23[lane][i] = cv[i];
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) h23[lane][8 + i] = R[51 + i];   // R[163..167]
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (bk == 3) {
            uint32_t B[5];
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = h23[lane][i];
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) B[i] = h23[lane][8 + i];
            ih_r17_mix_blocks<3, 163, 5, 11, 14>(R, B, Kw, tmw, cv, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
#endif
            // F = BLAKE3(tag(T_FINAL) || C || V || P || S || M): the 21-byte tag then the 44 prefix bytes 20..63 (C, V, P), S, M: 129 bytes
            b3_words_xof<129>(0, [&](uint32_t i) {
                if (i < 5) return tfw[i];
                // the tag's byte 20 then prefix[20 ..]: word 5 = tag byte 20 | prefix[20..22] << 8; word i (6..15) = prefix[4i - 1 .. 4i + 2] = pw[i-1] >> 24 | pw[i] << 8
                if (i == 5) return tfw[5] | (pw[5] << 8);
                if (i < 16) return (pw[i - 1] >> 24) | (pw[i] << 8);
                if (i == 16) return (pw[15] >> 24) | (S[0] << 8);
                if (i < 24) return (S[i - 17] >> 24) | (S[i - 16] << 8);
                if (i == 24) return (S[7] >> 24) | (M[0] << 8);
                if (i < 32) return (M[i - 25] >> 24) | (M[i - 24] << 8);
                return M[7] >> 24;
            }, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
            bool le = true;
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
                const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
                if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
                if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
            }
            if (le && live) {
                const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
                if (rs < max_results) {
                    uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                    e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
                }
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
}
#elif IH_R17
// VERSION 16, the static-fabric integer coordinate (pow/ref/src/igneumhash.cpp namespace r16f, the hash lead's text of 13 Oct 2026 15:1x UK; GPU A's
// port): one attempt per THREAD over 224 static registers (four banks of 56, the ilp4 chains independent), no dataset, no shuffles, no shared
// memory. S as every version; the 224 registers from BLAKE3xof(LP(init) || K || S, 896 bytes) = XOF blocks 0..13; the period's programme
// (rounds x 224 operations, compiled in as ih_r16_prog over R[224]); M = BLAKE3(LP(mix) || K || the 224 registers LE) = 947 bytes, one chunk; F the common final digest.
#ifndef IH_R17_REGS
#define IH_R17_REGS 224
#endif
#ifndef IH_R17_POOL
#define IH_R17_POOL 0
#endif
#ifndef IH_R17_MEM
#define IH_R17_MEM 0
#endif
#if IH_R17_POOL
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_r16_prog(unsigned int (&R)[IH_R17_REGS], const unsigned int* dg);
#elif IH_R17_MEM
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_r16_prog(unsigned int (&R)[IH_R17_REGS], const uint4* __restrict__ dag);
#else
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_r16_prog(unsigned int (&R)[IH_R17_REGS]);
#endif
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r17_tag(uint8_t* p, int which) {
    const char* t; uint32_t n;
    switch (which) { case 0: t = "IgneumHash/R0G/init"; n = 19; break; default: t = "IgneumHash/R0G/mix"; n = 18; break; }
    p[0] = (uint8_t)n;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[1 + i] = (uint8_t)t[i];
    return n + 1;
}
// revision 3's fold F(x; v) with the finaliser (r16f::fchain)
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r17_fold(uint32_t x, uint32_t v) { x = ih_rotl(x ^ v, 5u) + v; x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x9E3779B1u; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x85EBCA6Bu; x ^= x >> 13; return x; }
extern "C" __global__ void IH_SEARCH_BOUNDS
ih_search(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint4* __restrict__ dataset, uint32_t items_unused,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ scratch, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ results, uint32_t max_results) {
    const uint32_t slot = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const uint32_t slots = gridDim.x * blockDim.x;
    (void)scratch; (void)items_unused; (void)dataset;
    uint8_t buf[160];
    uint32_t o[16];
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, envelope, IH_PARAMS_LEN, 0, o);
    uint8_t prefix[96];
    uint32_t n = ih_tag(prefix, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) prefix[n + i] = envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + i];
    n += 12;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)o[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { const uint32_t w = params[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)w; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    const uint32_t Kw[8] = {IH_R16_K0, IH_R16_K1, IH_R16_K2, IH_R16_K3, IH_R16_K4, IH_R16_K5, IH_R16_K6, IH_R16_K7};
    // the init tag as words (20 bytes: len 19 || "IgneumHash/R0G/init"): the message tag || K || S is 84 bytes, word-aligned
    uint32_t tiw[5], tmw[5];
    { uint8_t b[24]; for (int k = 0; k < 24; ++k) b[k] = 0; ih_r17_tag(b, 0); for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) tiw[i] = (uint32_t)b[4 * i] | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 3] << 24); }
    // the mix tag (19 bytes: len 18 || "IgneumHash/R0G/mix"): K starts at byte 19, the registers at byte 51; word 4 carries tag bytes 16..18 and K's first byte
    { uint8_t b[24]; for (int k = 0; k < 24; ++k) b[k] = 0; ih_r17_tag(b, 1); for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) tmw[i] = (uint32_t)b[4 * i] | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 3] << 24); }
    for (uint32_t gi = slot; gi < count; gi += slots) {
        const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
        uint32_t S[8], M[8], F[8];
        for (int i = 0; i < 96; ++i) buf[i] = prefix[i];
        put_u64be(buf + 96, nonce);
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, 104, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
        unsigned int R[IH_R17_REGS];
#if IH_R17_TAILS
        // REVISION 3 (tails 1, pow/ref r16f under program_distance): the init ONE BLAKE3 of tag || K || S (84 bytes) to 32 bytes, expanded
        // by eight fold chains (register r = F(x[r mod 8]; r)); the mix the fold of the 224 registers into eight chains from K's words,
        // then ONE BLAKE3 of tag || K || x[0..8) (83 bytes)
        {
            b3_words_xof<84>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 5 ? tiw[i] : i < 13 ? Kw[i - 5] : S[i - 13]; }, o);
            uint32_t x[8];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) x[k] = o[k];
#pragma unroll
#if IH_R17_STREAM && !IH_R17_INPLACE
            // revisions 15/15b: W = N / 4 init values in the ring's registers N - W .. N - 1 (the stream's block 0 reads them), the rest zero
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t r = 0; r < (uint32_t)(IH_R17_REGS - IH_R17_REGS / 4); ++r) R[r] = 0u;
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t r = 0; r < (uint32_t)(IH_R17_REGS / 4); ++r) { x[r & 7u] = ih_r17_fold(x[r & 7u], r); R[IH_R17_REGS - IH_R17_REGS / 4 + r] = x[r & 7u]; }
#else
            for (uint32_t r = 0; r < (uint32_t)IH_R17_REGS; ++r) { x[r & 7u] = ih_r17_fold(x[r & 7u], r); R[r] = x[r & 7u]; }
#endif
        }
#if IH_R17_POOL
        ih_r16_prog(R, o);   // the segment pool: the init digest's bytes choose the segments
#elif IH_R17_MEM
        ih_r16_prog(R, dataset);   // revision 19: the epoch dataset's quarters fold into the stream's immediates
#else
        ih_r16_prog(R);
#endif
        {
            uint32_t x[8];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) x[k] = Kw[k];
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t r = 0; r < (uint32_t)IH_R17_REGS; ++r) x[r & 7u] = ih_r17_fold(x[r & 7u], R[r]);
            b3_words_xof<83>(0, [&](uint32_t i) {
                if (i < 4) return tmw[i];
                if (i == 4) return tmw[4] | (Kw[0] << 24);
                if (i < 12) return (Kw[i - 5] >> 8) | (Kw[i - 4] << 24);
                if (i == 12) return (Kw[7] >> 8) | (x[0] << 24);
                if (i < 20) return (x[i - 13] >> 8) | (x[i - 12] << 24);
                return x[7] >> 8;
            }, o);
        }
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
#else
#pragma unroll
        for (uint32_t t = 0; t < (uint32_t)(IH_R17_REGS / 16); ++t) {
            b3_words_xof<84>(t, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 5 ? tiw[i] : i < 13 ? Kw[i - 5] : S[i - 13]; }, o);
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < 16u; ++k) R[16u * t + k] = o[k];
        }
        ih_r16_prog(R);
        // M = BLAKE3(LP(mix) || K || R[0..224) LE): 19 + 32 + 896 = 947 bytes (237 words), one chunk, from words in registers
        b3_words_xof<51 + 4 * IH_R17_REGS>(0, [&](uint32_t i) {
            if (i < 4) return tmw[i];
            if (i == 4) return tmw[4] | (Kw[0] << 24);
            if (i < 12) return (Kw[i - 5] >> 8) | (Kw[i - 4] << 24);   // K's byte j at message byte 19 + j: word 4 ends with K byte 0, word 5 + j carries K bytes 4j + 1 .. 4j + 4
            if (i == 12) return (Kw[7] >> 8) | (R[0] << 24);
            if (i < 12u + (uint32_t)IH_R17_REGS) return (R[i - 13] >> 8) | (R[i - 12] << 24);
            return R[IH_R17_REGS - 1] >> 8;
        }, o);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
#endif
        uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
        for (int i = 0; i < 44; ++i) buf[fn + i] = prefix[20 + i];
        fn += 44;
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
        fn += 32;
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
        fn += 32;
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
        bool le = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
            const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
            if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
            if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
        }
        if (le) {
            const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
            if (rs < max_results) {
                uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
            }
        }
    }
}
#elif IH_R0H
// VERSION 17, THE TEXTURE FORM (pow/ref r17): one attempt per thread. The static 4096 x 4096 R32_UINT texture (texel (x, y) =
// fmix32(y * 4096 + x), built once on the host) is sampled through the texture unit with NEAREST filtering at the texel centre
// (x + 0.5, y + 0.5), clamp addressing, unnormalised coordinates. Per attempt: S the work seed as every version; st[0..7] the eight
// LE words of BLAKE3(LP("IgneumHash/R0H/init") || K || S); for i in 0 .. ROUNDS - 1: l = i & 7, c = st[l] ^ Kw[(i >> 3) & 7],
// x = c & 4095, y = (c >> 12) & 4095, t = tex2D(x, y), out[i] = t, st[l] = fmix32(st[l] ^ t ^ (i * 0x9E3779B1));
// M = BLAKE3(LP("IgneumHash/R0H/mix") || K || out[0..ROUNDS) LE); F the common final digest over S and M.
#ifndef IH_ROUNDS
#define IH_ROUNDS 512
#endif
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r0h_tag(uint8_t* p, int which) {
    const char* t; uint32_t n;
    switch (which) { case 0: t = "IgneumHash/R0H/init"; n = 19; break; default: t = "IgneumHash/R0H/mix"; n = 18; break; }
    p[0] = (uint8_t)n;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[1 + i] = (uint8_t)t[i];
    return n + 1;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r0h_fmix32(uint32_t h) { h ^= h >> 16; h *= 0x85EBCA6Bu; h ^= h >> 13; h *= 0xC2B2AE35u; h ^= h >> 16; return h; }
// BLAKE3 over a word-indexed message longer than one chunk (the texture form's mix is 51 + 4 * ROUNDS bytes: 2,099 at s512,
// 16,435 at s4096): each 1024-byte chunk compressed with its counter (CHUNK_START on its first block, CHUNK_END on its last),
// the chunk values merged by the reference's stack rule (merge while the chunk count's low bit is clear), the last chunk's
// value folded against the stack from the top with the final parent carrying ROOT (the single-chunk case is b3_words_xof's).
template <uint32_t LEN, class Get>
__device__ __forceinline__ void b3_words_multi(Get get, uint32_t out8[8]) {
    constexpr uint32_t nwords = (LEN + 3u) / 4u;
    constexpr uint32_t nchunks = (LEN + 1023u) / 1024u;
    uint32_t stack[8][8]; uint32_t sp = 0;
    uint32_t last[8];
    for (uint32_t c = 0; c < nchunks; ++c) {
        uint32_t cv[8];
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
        const uint32_t cbytes = (c + 1 == nchunks) ? (LEN - 1024u * c) : 1024u;
        const uint32_t nblocks = cbytes == 0 ? 1u : (cbytes + 63u) / 64u;
        uint32_t o[16];
        for (uint32_t b = 0; b < nblocks; ++b) {
            uint32_t m[16];
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 16; ++i) { const uint32_t w = 256u * c + 16u * b + i; m[i] = w < nwords ? get(w) : 0u; }
            const uint32_t blen = (b + 1 == nblocks) ? (cbytes - 64u * b) : 64u;
            uint32_t flags = (b == 0 ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u) | (b + 1 == nblocks ? B3_CHUNK_END : 0u);
            if (nchunks == 1 && b + 1 == nblocks) flags |= B3_ROOT;
            b3_compress_ctr(cv, m, (uint64_t)c, blen, flags, o);
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = o[i];
        }
        if (nchunks == 1) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) out8[i] = cv[i];
            return;
        }
        if (c + 1 == nchunks) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) last[i] = cv[i];
        } else {
            // add the chunk value to the stack: merge while the count of chunks so far has its low bit clear
            uint32_t n = c + 1;
            uint32_t v[8];
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) v[i] = cv[i];
            while ((n & 1u) == 0u) {
                uint32_t m[16], iv[8], po[16];
#pragma unroll
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { iv[i] = c_b3_iv[i]; m[i] = stack[sp - 1][i]; m[8 + i] = v[i]; }
                b3_compress_ctr(iv, m, 0, 64, B3_PARENT, po);
#pragma unroll
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) v[i] = po[i];
                --sp; n >>= 1;
            }
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) stack[sp][i] = v[i];
            ++sp;
        }
    }
    // fold the last chunk's value against the stack from the top; the final parent is the root
    uint32_t v[8];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) v[i] = last[i];
    while (sp > 0) {
        uint32_t m[16], iv[8], po[16];
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { iv[i] = c_b3_iv[i]; m[i] = stack[sp - 1][i]; m[8 + i] = v[i]; }
        b3_compress_ctr(iv, m, 0, 64, B3_PARENT | (sp == 1 ? B3_ROOT : 0u), po);
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) v[i] = po[i];
        --sp;
    }
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) out8[i] = v[i];
}
extern "C" __global__ void IH_SEARCH_BOUNDS
ih_search(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint4* __restrict__ dataset, uint32_t items_unused,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ scratch, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ results, uint32_t max_results, unsigned long long tex_handle) {
    const uint32_t slot = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const uint32_t slots = gridDim.x * blockDim.x;
    (void)scratch; (void)items_unused; (void)dataset;
    const cudaTextureObject_t tex = (cudaTextureObject_t)tex_handle;
    uint8_t buf[160];
    uint32_t o[16];
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, envelope, IH_PARAMS_LEN, 0, o);
    uint8_t prefix[96];
    uint32_t n = ih_tag(prefix, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) prefix[n + i] = envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + i];
    n += 12;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)o[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { const uint32_t w = params[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)w; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    const uint32_t Kw[8] = {IH_R16_K0, IH_R16_K1, IH_R16_K2, IH_R16_K3, IH_R16_K4, IH_R16_K5, IH_R16_K6, IH_R16_K7};
    uint32_t tiw[5], tmw[5];
    { uint8_t b[24]; for (int k = 0; k < 24; ++k) b[k] = 0; ih_r0h_tag(b, 0); for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) tiw[i] = (uint32_t)b[4 * i] | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 3] << 24); }
    { uint8_t b[24]; for (int k = 0; k < 24; ++k) b[k] = 0; ih_r0h_tag(b, 1); for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) tmw[i] = (uint32_t)b[4 * i] | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 3] << 24); }
    for (uint32_t gi = slot; gi < count; gi += slots) {
        const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
        uint32_t S[8], M[8], F[8];
        for (int i = 0; i < 96; ++i) buf[i] = prefix[i];
        put_u64be(buf + 96, nonce);
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, 104, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
        // st = the eight LE words of BLAKE3(LP(init) || K || S): 20 + 32 + 32 = 84 bytes, one chunk
        uint32_t st[8];
        b3_words_xof<84>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 5 ? tiw[i] : i < 13 ? Kw[i - 5] : S[i - 13]; }, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) st[k] = o[k];
        uint32_t out[IH_ROUNDS];
#pragma unroll 8
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS; ++i) {
            const uint32_t l = i & 7u, cw = st[l] ^ Kw[(i >> 3) & 7u];
            const uint32_t x = cw & 4095u, y = (cw >> 12) & 4095u;
            const uint32_t t = tex2D<unsigned int>(tex, (float)x + 0.5f, (float)y + 0.5f);
            out[i] = t;
            st[l] = ih_r0h_fmix32(st[l] ^ t ^ (i * 0x9E3779B1u));
        }
        // M = BLAKE3(LP(mix) || K || out[0..ROUNDS) LE): 19 + 32 + 4 * ROUNDS bytes; K at byte 19, the outputs at byte 51 (the v16 packing)
        b3_words_multi<51 + 4 * IH_ROUNDS>([&](uint32_t i) {
            if (i < 4) return tmw[i];
            if (i == 4) return tmw[4] | (Kw[0] << 24);
            if (i < 12) return (Kw[i - 5] >> 8) | (Kw[i - 4] << 24);
            if (i == 12) return (Kw[7] >> 8) | (out[0] << 24);
            if (i < 12u + (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS) return (out[i - 13] >> 8) | (out[i - 12] << 24);
            return out[IH_ROUNDS - 1] >> 8;
        }, M);
        uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
        for (int i = 0; i < 44; ++i) buf[fn + i] = prefix[20 + i];
        fn += 44;
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
        fn += 32;
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
        fn += 32;
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
        bool le = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
            const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
            if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
            if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
        }
        if (le) {
            const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
            if (rs < max_results) {
                uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
            }
        }
    }
}
#elif IH_R0I
// VERSION 18, THE RASTER FORM (pow/ref r18): one attempt per thread, the rasteriser in software (the reference's exact integer edge
// functions and top-left rule, y down, ONE + ONE ADD saturating RGBA8). Per attempt: S; st[0..7] the eight LE words of
// BLAKE3(LP("IgneumHash/R0I/init") || K || S); triangle i from the word w = st[l] ^ Kw[(i >> 3) & 7] (l = i & 7) by the probe's
// derivation, st[l] = fmix32(st[l] ^ colour ^ (i * 0x9E3779B1)); M = BLAKE3(LP("IgneumHash/R0I/mix") || K || every triangle's
// bounding box in order, row-major RGBA, after every triangle is drawn). Saturating addition of non-negative bytes is order-free
// (min(255, the sum)), so a box pixel's value is min(255, the sum over every triangle covering it): the thread keeps no framebuffer,
// it evaluates each box pixel against the triangles whose boxes can reach it (anchors within 30 pixels on both axes). The digest is a
// streaming BLAKE3 (the message length is data-dependent): the chunk state, the chaining-value stack and the parent tree as the
// standard's reference hasher.
#ifndef IH_ROUNDS
#define IH_ROUNDS 1024
#endif
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r0i_tag(uint8_t* p, int which) {
    const char* t; uint32_t n;
    switch (which) { case 0: t = "IgneumHash/R0I/init"; n = 19; break; default: t = "IgneumHash/R0I/mix"; n = 18; break; }
    p[0] = (uint8_t)n;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[1 + i] = (uint8_t)t[i];
    return n + 1;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r0i_fmix32(uint32_t h) { h ^= h >> 16; h *= 0x85EBCA6Bu; h ^= h >> 13; h *= 0xC2B2AE35u; h ^= h >> 16; return h; }
// the streaming BLAKE3 hasher (bytes in, 32 bytes out), the standard's chunk state and chaining-value stack
struct ih_b3s {
    uint32_t cv[8];
    uint32_t block[16];
    uint32_t block_len;
    uint32_t blocks_compressed;
    uint64_t chunk_counter;
    uint32_t stack[14][8];
    uint32_t stack_len;
};
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_b3s_init(ih_b3s& h) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h.cv[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) h.block[i] = 0u;
    h.block_len = 0; h.blocks_compressed = 0; h.chunk_counter = 0; h.stack_len = 0;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_b3s_parent(const uint32_t l[8], const uint32_t r[8], uint32_t flags, uint32_t out8[8]) {
    uint32_t m[16], iv[8], o[16];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { iv[i] = c_b3_iv[i]; m[i] = l[i]; m[8 + i] = r[i]; }
    b3_compress_ctr(iv, m, 0, 64, B3_PARENT | flags, o);
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) out8[i] = o[i];
}
// one byte into the chunk state (the block compressed only when full and another byte follows; a full chunk closed when another byte follows)
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_b3s_byte(ih_b3s& h, uint8_t b) {
    if (h.blocks_compressed == 15u && h.block_len == 64u) {   // 15 compressed blocks and a full buffered 16th = the 1024-byte chunk
        // the chunk is complete and more input follows: close it and add its chaining value to the stack
        uint32_t o[16];
        b3_compress_ctr(h.cv, h.block, h.chunk_counter, 64, (h.blocks_compressed == 0u ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u) | B3_CHUNK_END, o);
        uint32_t ncv[8];
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) ncv[i] = o[i];
        uint64_t total = h.chunk_counter + 1u;
        while ((total & 1u) == 0u) { uint32_t t[8]; ih_b3s_parent(h.stack[h.stack_len - 1], ncv, 0u, t); --h.stack_len;
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) ncv[i] = t[i]; total >>= 1; }
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h.stack[h.stack_len][i] = ncv[i];
        ++h.stack_len;
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h.cv[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) h.block[i] = 0u;
        h.block_len = 0; h.blocks_compressed = 0; ++h.chunk_counter;
    } else if (h.block_len == 64u) {
        // the block is full and more input follows within the chunk
        uint32_t o[16];
        b3_compress_ctr(h.cv, h.block, h.chunk_counter, 64, h.blocks_compressed == 0u ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h.cv[i] = o[i];
        ++h.blocks_compressed;
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) h.block[i] = 0u;
        h.block_len = 0;
    }
    h.block[h.block_len >> 2] |= ((uint32_t)b) << (8u * (h.block_len & 3u));
    ++h.block_len;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_b3s_word(ih_b3s& h, uint32_t w) { ih_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)w); ih_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)(w >> 8)); ih_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)(w >> 16)); ih_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)(w >> 24)); }
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_b3s_final(ih_b3s& h, uint32_t out8[8]) {
    uint32_t o[16];
    const uint32_t flags = (h.blocks_compressed == 0u ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u) | B3_CHUNK_END | (h.stack_len == 0u ? B3_ROOT : 0u);
    b3_compress_ctr(h.cv, h.block, h.chunk_counter, h.block_len, flags, o);
    uint32_t v[8];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) v[i] = o[i];
    uint32_t rem = h.stack_len;
    while (rem > 0u) { uint32_t t[8]; ih_b3s_parent(h.stack[rem - 1], v, rem == 1u ? B3_ROOT : 0u, t);
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) v[i] = t[i]; --rem; }
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) out8[i] = v[i];
}
// the triangle of a state word (the probe's derivation): vertices as integer pixel corners clamped to 0 .. 8192, the colour word
struct ih_r0i_tri { int x0, y0, x1, y1, x2, y2; uint32_t rgba; };
__device__ __forceinline__ int ih_r0i_clamp(int v) { return v < 0 ? 0 : v > 8192 ? 8192 : v; }
__device__ __forceinline__ ih_r0i_tri ih_r0i_triangle(uint32_t w) {
    const uint32_t u = ih_r0i_fmix32(w ^ 0x2545F491u), c = ih_r0i_fmix32(w ^ 0x9E3779B9u);
    ih_r0i_tri r; r.x0 = (int)(w & 8191u); r.y0 = (int)((w >> 13) & 8191u);
    r.x1 = ih_r0i_clamp(r.x0 + (int)(u & 31u) - 15); r.y1 = ih_r0i_clamp(r.y0 + (int)((u >> 5) & 31u) - 15);
    r.x2 = ih_r0i_clamp(r.x0 + (int)((u >> 10) & 31u) - 15); r.y2 = ih_r0i_clamp(r.y0 + (int)((u >> 15) & 31u) - 15);
    r.rgba = c; return r;
}
// does triangle t cover pixel (i, j)? the reference's exact integer edge functions at the pixel centre (2i + 1, 2j + 1) with the top-left rule
__device__ __forceinline__ bool ih_r0i_covers(const ih_r0i_tri& t, int i, int j) {
    const long long ax = t.x0, ay = t.y0, bx = t.x1, by = t.y1, cx = t.x2, cy = t.y2;
    const long long area = (bx - ax) * (cy - ay) - (by - ay) * (cx - ax);
    if (area == 0) return false;
    const long long s = area > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const long long px = 2 * (long long)i + 1, py = 2 * (long long)j + 1;
    const long long ex[3] = {ax, bx, cx}, ey[3] = {ay, by, cy}, fx[3] = {bx, cx, ax}, fy[3] = {by, cy, ay};
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
        const long long dx = fx[k] - ex[k], dy = fy[k] - ey[k];
        const bool tl = (dy == 0) ? ((dx > 0 ? 1 : -1) == s) : ((-dy > 0 ? 1 : -1) == s);
        const long long w = ((2 * fx[k] - 2 * ex[k]) * (py - 2 * ey[k]) - (2 * fy[k] - 2 * ey[k]) * (px - 2 * ex[k])) * s;
        if (w < 0 || (w == 0 && !tl)) return false;
    }
    return true;
}
extern "C" __global__ void IH_SEARCH_BOUNDS
ih_search(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint4* __restrict__ dataset, uint32_t items_unused,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ scratch, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ results, uint32_t max_results, unsigned long long tex_unused) {
    const uint32_t slot = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const uint32_t slots = gridDim.x * blockDim.x;
    (void)scratch; (void)items_unused; (void)dataset; (void)tex_unused;
    uint8_t buf[160];
    uint32_t o[16];
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, envelope, IH_PARAMS_LEN, 0, o);
    uint8_t prefix[96];
    uint32_t n = ih_tag(prefix, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) prefix[n + i] = envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + i];
    n += 12;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)o[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { const uint32_t w = params[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)w; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    const uint32_t Kw[8] = {IH_R16_K0, IH_R16_K1, IH_R16_K2, IH_R16_K3, IH_R16_K4, IH_R16_K5, IH_R16_K6, IH_R16_K7};
    uint32_t tiw[5];
    uint8_t tmix[24];
    { uint8_t b[24]; for (int k = 0; k < 24; ++k) b[k] = 0; ih_r0i_tag(b, 0); for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) tiw[i] = (uint32_t)b[4 * i] | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)b[4 * i + 3] << 24); }
    for (int k = 0; k < 24; ++k) tmix[k] = 0; ih_r0i_tag(tmix, 1);
    for (uint32_t gi = slot; gi < count; gi += slots) {
        const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
        uint32_t S[8], M[8], F[8];
        for (int i = 0; i < 96; ++i) buf[i] = prefix[i];
        put_u64be(buf + 96, nonce);
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, 104, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
        uint32_t st[8];
        b3_words_xof<84>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 5 ? tiw[i] : i < 13 ? Kw[i - 5] : S[i - 13]; }, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) st[k] = o[k];
        // the triangles' words in order (each triangle recomputed from its word where needed)
        uint32_t tw[IH_ROUNDS];
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS; ++i) {
            const uint32_t l = i & 7u, w = st[l] ^ Kw[(i >> 3) & 7u];
            tw[i] = w;
            const uint32_t colour = ih_r0i_fmix32(w ^ 0x9E3779B9u);
            st[l] = ih_r0i_fmix32(st[l] ^ colour ^ (i * 0x9E3779B1u));
        }
        // the digest: mix tag || K || the boxes in order after every triangle is drawn
        ih_b3s hs; ih_b3s_init(hs);
        for (int k = 0; k < 19; ++k) ih_b3s_byte(hs, tmix[k]);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) ih_b3s_word(hs, Kw[k]);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS; ++i) {
            const ih_r0i_tri t = ih_r0i_triangle(tw[i]);
            const int minx = min(t.x0, min(t.x1, t.x2)), maxx = max(t.x0, max(t.x1, t.x2));
            const int miny = min(t.y0, min(t.y1, t.y2)), maxy = max(t.y0, max(t.y1, t.y2));
            if (minx >= maxx || miny >= maxy) continue;
            // the candidates: every triangle whose box can reach this one (anchors within 30 on both axes)
            for (int j = miny; j < maxy; ++j) for (int x = minx; x < maxx; ++x) {
                uint32_t r = 0, g = 0, b = 0, a = 0;
                for (uint32_t c = 0; c < (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS; ++c) {
                    const uint32_t wc = tw[c];
                    const int cx0 = (int)(wc & 8191u), cy0 = (int)((wc >> 13) & 8191u);
                    if (cx0 - t.x0 > 32 || t.x0 - cx0 > 32 || cy0 - t.y0 > 32 || t.y0 - cy0 > 32) continue;   // offsets run -15 .. +16, so boxes can meet at 31
                    const ih_r0i_tri tc = ih_r0i_triangle(wc);
                    if (x < min(tc.x0, min(tc.x1, tc.x2)) || x >= max(tc.x0, max(tc.x1, tc.x2)) || j < min(tc.y0, min(tc.y1, tc.y2)) || j >= max(tc.y0, max(tc.y1, tc.y2))) continue;
                    if (!ih_r0i_covers(tc, x, j)) continue;
                    r += tc.rgba & 255u; g += (tc.rgba >> 8) & 255u; b += (tc.rgba >> 16) & 255u; a += tc.rgba >> 24;
                }
                const uint32_t px = min(r, 255u) | (min(g, 255u) << 8) | (min(b, 255u) << 16) | (min(a, 255u) << 24);
                ih_b3s_word(hs, px);
            }
        }
        ih_b3s_final(hs, M);
        uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
        for (int i = 0; i < 44; ++i) buf[fn + i] = prefix[20 + i];
        fn += 44;
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
        fn += 32;
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
        fn += 32;
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
        bool le = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
            const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
            if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
            if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
        }
        if (le) {
            const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
            if (rs < max_results) {
                uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
            }
        }
    }
}
#elif IH_FORGE || IH_FORGE2
// VERSION 20, FORGE (pow/ref namespace forge; the founder's order 14 Oct 2026 22:3x UK; the panel's reading 8) and VERSION 21,
// ANVIL / FORGE2 (pow/ref namespace forge2, FORM-FORGE.md section 7, the two-level form): one attempt per warp, lanes 0..15 the 16
// program lanes (16..31 idle), each lane R[32] in registers (slot k writes register k and reads 7k+1 and 11k+5 mod 32 -- STATIC
// operands when the slot loop is unrolled), its 1 KiB scratchpad in local memory, XLANE by a warp shuffle within lanes 0..15, the
// keyed 256-byte read of the 1 GiB dataset every IH_FG_FOLD steps folded across four lanes, the FP32-RN classes under the
// exponent mask. FORGE: the hash's own 32 words (fixed or drawn quota order), the mix over the programme's hash. FORGE2: the
// BATCH's IH_FG_WORDS shapes from the period seed BLAKE3(period || E || header || LE64(nonce >> IH_FG2_BL)), each shape's class
// order drawn; the HASH's IH_FG2_SEQ-entry sequence over them and its own words (the scratchpad addresses and the cmov
// predicate), the mix2 over the period seed and the sequence bytes. This is the interpretive form (the shapes drawn in the
// kernel per hash): exact by construction against forge2::attempt; the host-compiled per-batch form (the shapes baked as a
// switch, the batch's module NVRTC-compiled overlapped) is the figure form and follows. S and the final digest are the common
// R0A seed and digest; only the middle (producing M) is the form's.
#ifndef IH_FG2_FPMODE
#define IH_FG2_FPMODE 0   // the document's fp_mode: 0 as cut, 1 integer-only, 2 producer-masked (the FP bank r24..r31), 3 the occupancy form (integer-only, the wiring k+21 / k+9, the scratchpad 16 words a lane)
#endif
#if IH_FG2_FPMODE == 3
#define IH_FG2_SPW 16u
#define IH_FG2_SPMASK 63u
#else
#define IH_FG2_SPW 256u
#define IH_FG2_SPMASK 1023u
#endif
#ifndef IH_FG2_APW2
#define IH_FG2_APW2 0   // the occupancy port: two attempts a warp (lanes 0..15 and 16..31, the halves in lockstep; the shuffles segment at width 16)
#endif
#ifndef IH_FG2_SPSHARED
#define IH_FG2_SPSHARED 0   // the occupancy port: the lane's scratchpad in shared memory (IH_BLOCK x IH_FG2_SPW words)
#endif
#if IH_FG2_APW2
#define FG_WMASK 0xFFFFFFFFu
#else
#define FG_WMASK 0xFFFFu
#endif
#ifndef IH_FORGE2_COMPILED
#define IH_FORGE2_COMPILED 0   // 1 when the host prepends the batch's generated shapes (forge2_shapes.hpp: fg2_dispatch); 0 the interpretive form
#endif
enum { FGFMA = 0, FGFADDSUB, FGFMUL, FGIALU, FGROT, FGMUL, FGMULHI, FGI2F, FGF2I, FGXLANE, FGSTORE, FGCMOV };
__device__ const unsigned char c_fg_quota_int[32] = { FGIALU, FGIALU, FGROT, FGIALU, FGXLANE, FGIALU, FGIALU, FGMUL, FGIALU, FGROT, FGROT, FGIALU, FGSTORE, FGIALU, FGIALU, FGROT, FGMULHI, FGIALU, FGIALU, FGMUL, FGROT, FGIALU, FGXLANE, FGXLANE, FGIALU, FGIALU, FGROT, FGCMOV, FGSTORE, FGIALU, FGIALU, FGMUL };   // fp_mode 1 and 3: the ten FP slots to IALU 5, ROT 2, MUL 1, XLANE 1, STORE 1
__device__ const unsigned char c_fg_quota[32] = { FGIALU, FGFMA, FGROT, FGIALU, FGXLANE, FGFADDSUB, FGIALU, FGMUL, FGIALU, FGFMUL, FGROT, FGIALU, FGSTORE, FGFMA, FGIALU, FGROT, FGMULHI, FGFADDSUB, FGIALU, FGI2F, FGROT, FGIALU, FGXLANE, FGFMUL, FGIALU, FGFMA, FGROT, FGCMOV, FGFADDSUB, FGIALU, FGF2I, FGMUL };
// the fold F(x; v) with the finaliser (r16f::fchain; identical to the version 16 block's ih_r17q_fold, which this branch does not see)
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t fg_fold(uint32_t x, uint32_t v) { x = ih_rotl(x ^ v, 5u) + v; x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x9E3779B1u; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x85EBCA6Bu; x ^= x >> 13; return x; }
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t fg_mask(uint32_t u) { const uint32_t e = (u >> 23) & 255u; const uint32_t e2 = (e == 0u || e == 255u) ? 127u : (e < 111u ? 111u : (e > 143u ? 143u : e)); return (u & 0x807FFFFFu) | (e2 << 23); }
// the five length-prefixed tags: 0 program, 1 init, 2 mix (forge), 3 period, 4 mix2 (forge2)
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t fg_tag(uint8_t* p, int which) {
    const char* t; uint32_t n;
    switch (which) { case 0: t = "IgneumHash/Forge/program"; n = 24; break; case 1: t = "IgneumHash/Forge/init"; n = 21; break; case 2: t = "IgneumHash/Forge/mix"; n = 20; break; case 3: t = "IgneumHash/Forge/period"; n = 23; break; case 5: t = "IgneumHash/Forge/sequence"; n = 25; break; default: t = "IgneumHash/Forge/mix2"; n = 21; break; }
    p[0] = (uint8_t)n; for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[1 + i] = (uint8_t)t[i]; return n + 1;
}
// the streaming BLAKE3 hasher (bytes/words in, 32 bytes out), the standard's chunk state and chaining-value stack (identical
// to the raster form's ih_b3s; forge's mix is 18,517 bytes = 19 chunks, a balanced tree)
struct fg_b3s { uint32_t cv[8]; uint32_t block[16]; uint32_t block_len; uint32_t blocks_compressed; uint64_t chunk_counter; uint32_t stack[14][8]; uint32_t stack_len; };
__device__ __forceinline__ void fg_b3s_init(fg_b3s& h) {
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h.cv[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) h.block[i] = 0u;
    h.block_len = 0; h.blocks_compressed = 0; h.chunk_counter = 0; h.stack_len = 0;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void fg_b3s_parent(const uint32_t l[8], const uint32_t r[8], uint32_t flags, uint32_t out8[8]) {
    uint32_t m[16], iv[8], o[16];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { iv[i] = c_b3_iv[i]; m[i] = l[i]; m[8 + i] = r[i]; }
    b3_compress_ctr(iv, m, 0, 64, B3_PARENT | flags, o);
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) out8[i] = o[i];
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void fg_b3s_byte(fg_b3s& h, uint8_t b) {
    if (h.blocks_compressed == 15u && h.block_len == 64u) {
        uint32_t o[16];
        b3_compress_ctr(h.cv, h.block, h.chunk_counter, 64, (h.blocks_compressed == 0u ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u) | B3_CHUNK_END, o);
        uint32_t ncv[8];
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) ncv[i] = o[i];
        uint64_t total = h.chunk_counter + 1u;
        while ((total & 1u) == 0u) { uint32_t t[8]; fg_b3s_parent(h.stack[h.stack_len - 1], ncv, 0u, t); --h.stack_len;
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) ncv[i] = t[i]; total >>= 1; }
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h.stack[h.stack_len][i] = ncv[i];
        ++h.stack_len;
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h.cv[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) h.block[i] = 0u;
        h.block_len = 0; h.blocks_compressed = 0; ++h.chunk_counter;
    } else if (h.block_len == 64u) {
        uint32_t o[16];
        b3_compress_ctr(h.cv, h.block, h.chunk_counter, 64, h.blocks_compressed == 0u ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h.cv[i] = o[i];
        ++h.blocks_compressed;
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) h.block[i] = 0u;
        h.block_len = 0;
    }
    h.block[h.block_len >> 2] |= ((uint32_t)b) << (8u * (h.block_len & 3u));
    ++h.block_len;
}
__device__ __forceinline__ void fg_b3s_word(fg_b3s& h, uint32_t w) { fg_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)w); fg_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)(w >> 8)); fg_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)(w >> 16)); fg_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)(w >> 24)); }
__device__ __forceinline__ void fg_b3s_final(fg_b3s& h, uint32_t out8[8]) {
    uint32_t o[16];
    const uint32_t flags = (h.blocks_compressed == 0u ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u) | B3_CHUNK_END | (h.stack_len == 0u ? B3_ROOT : 0u);
    b3_compress_ctr(h.cv, h.block, h.chunk_counter, h.block_len, flags, o);
    uint32_t v[8];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) v[i] = o[i];
    uint32_t rem = h.stack_len;
    while (rem > 0u) { uint32_t t[8]; fg_b3s_parent(h.stack[rem - 1], v, rem == 1u ? B3_ROOT : 0u, t);
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) v[i] = t[i]; --rem; }
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) out8[i] = v[i];
}
#ifndef IH_FG3
#define IH_FG3 0
#endif
#if IH_FG3
// THE WHOLE-ENTRY COMPILED FORM (GPU C, 15 October 2026): three kernels an attempt batch, the step work alone on the search kernel.
//   ih_fg3_prep: one thread an attempt: S, the hash's seed, the period seed, the sequence and the hash's words, the init chain
//                (R, SP or WN) into the state buffer (no lane replays the chain sixteen times over);
//   ih_search:   one attempt a warp (lanes 0..15; IH_FG3_APW 2: two, the halves in lockstep), the batch's shapes as whole-entry
//                bodies (fg3_dispatch, generated on the host), the keyed read inside the body, the state read and written back;
//   ih_fg3_mix:  four lanes an attempt, one BLAKE3 chunk each (the mix2 message is 3,190 bytes under the 16-word scratchpad or
//                the window), lane 0 of the four the parent tree, the final digest and the target compare.
// The state buffer's layout an attempt (words): S 8 | pseed 8 | seq (L bytes, a uint4 multiple of words) | hw L and the 8 seed words | R 512 as [lane][register] | SP 16 x
// 16 as [lane][word] | WN 256 as [lane][word] under fp_mode 6 (the init chain's own order: the prep writes it sequentially in uint4s).
#if IH_FG3_FPMODE == 6
#define FG3_SPW 0u
#define FG3_WNW 16u
#elif IH_FG3_FPMODE == 9
#define FG3_SPW 0u
#define FG3_WNW 0u
#else
#define FG3_SPW 16u   // fp_mode 3 and 4: the 64-byte lane scratchpad
#define FG3_WNW 0u
#endif
#define FG3_OFF_S 0u
#define FG3_OFF_PSEED 8u
#define FG3_OFF_SEQ 16u
#define FG3_SEQW4 ((((IH_FG2_SEQ + 3u) / 4u) + 3u) & ~3u)   // the sequence's words, rounded to a uint4
#define FG3_OFF_HW (16u + FG3_SEQW4)
#define FG3_HWW (((IH_FG2_SEQ + 8u) + 3u) & ~3u)   // the hash's words (L of them) and the eight seed words (select-all, chain_select), rounded to a uint4
#define FG3_OFF_R (FG3_OFF_HW + FG3_HWW)
#define FG3_OFF_SP (FG3_OFF_R + 512u)
#define FG3_OFF_WN (FG3_OFF_SP + 16u * FG3_SPW)
#define FG3_OFF_KX (FG3_OFF_WN + 16u * FG3_WNW)   // the saved prefetch word a lane (the regrouping form)
#ifndef IH_FG3_KEYED
#define IH_FG3_KEYED 0u   // F-441 / F-504 (KEYED-RELEASED-VERSIONS-SPEC.md sections 2 and 9): the bond-keyed item-read steps S on version 21 (0 = the unkeyed document)
#endif
#define FG3_OFF_KS (FG3_OFF_KX + 16u)   // the shares s_0 .. s_{S-1}, eight words each, written by the search at the keyed read steps, read by the mix
#define FG3_STATE_WORDS (FG3_OFF_KS + 8u * IH_FG3_KEYED)
#define FG3_OFF_Z (FG3_OFF_HW + IH_FG2_SEQ)   // the seed words
#define FG3_SEQW (IH_FG2_SEQ / 4u)   // the sequence's words in the stream (L bytes, L a multiple of 4)
#ifndef IH_FG2_LEAN
#define IH_FG2_LEAN 0
#endif
#if IH_FG2_LEAN
#define FG3_LANE_BYTES 256u   // the lean mix: the 16 lanes' 16-byte accumulators
#define FG3_MSG_BYTES (22u + 64u + IH_FG2_SEQ + FG3_LANE_BYTES + 32u * IH_FG3_KEYED)   // tag(21) || E || pseed || seq (L bytes) || the accumulators || the shares whole (F-504 (i)+)
#else
#define FG3_LANE_BYTES (2048u + 64u * FG3_SPW + 64u * FG3_WNW)   // R || SP || WN
#define FG3_MSG_BYTES (22u + 64u + IH_FG2_SEQ + FG3_LANE_BYTES + 32u * IH_FG3_KEYED)   // tag(21) || E || pseed || seq (L bytes) || R || SP || WN || the shares whole (F-504 (i)+)
#endif
#define FG3_V_WORDS ((FG3_MSG_BYTES - 22u) / 4u)
#define FG3_V_R (16u + FG3_SEQW)   // the stream word where R begins
#define FG3_V_KS (FG3_V_R + FG3_LANE_BYTES / 4u)   // the stream word where the shares begin (the lanes' words end)
#define FG3_CHUNKS ((FG3_MSG_BYTES + 1023u) / 1024u)
#if FG3_CHUNKS <= 4
#define FG3_G 4
#elif FG3_CHUNKS <= 8
#define FG3_G 8
#elif FG3_CHUNKS <= 16
#define FG3_G 16
#else
#define FG3_G 32
#endif
// the common prefix (R0A seed domain): tag(0) || header[12] || BLAKE3(params) || params[0..8]
__device__ __forceinline__ void fg3_prefix(const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope, uint8_t prefix[96]) {
    uint32_t o[16];
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, envelope, IH_PARAMS_LEN, 0, o);
    uint32_t n = ih_tag(prefix, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) prefix[n + i] = envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + i];
    n += 12;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)o[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { const uint32_t w = params[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)w; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
}
extern "C" __global__ void __launch_bounds__(128)
ih_fg3_prep(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope, uint32_t* __restrict__ state) {
    const uint32_t gi = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (gi >= count) return;
    const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
    uint32_t* st = state + (size_t)gi * FG3_STATE_WORDS;
    uint4* st4 = (uint4*)st;   // every store a 16-byte one in sequence: the two halves of a sector written back to back
    const uint8_t* Eb = envelope + IH_PARAMS_LEN + 12;
    uint8_t prefix[96]; fg3_prefix(params, envelope, prefix);
    uint32_t o[16];
    uint8_t buf[128];
    for (int i = 0; i < 96; ++i) buf[i] = prefix[i];
    put_u64be(buf + 96, nonce);
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, 104, 0, o);
    uint32_t S[8];
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
    st4[0] = make_uint4(S[0], S[1], S[2], S[3]); st4[1] = make_uint4(S[4], S[5], S[6], S[7]);
    // the hash's seed = BLAKE3(LP(program) || E || S)
    uint32_t pn = fg_tag(buf, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) buf[pn + i] = Eb[i]; pn += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[pn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[pn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[pn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[pn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); } pn += 32;
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, pn, 0, o);
    uint32_t y[8];
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) y[k] = o[k];
#if IH_FG3_FPMODE == 9 || IH_FG3_CSEL
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) st[FG3_OFF_Z + (uint32_t)k] = o[k];   // the hash's seed words (the select-all step chain; chain_select's first read word)
#endif
    // the period seed = BLAKE3(LP(period) || E || header || LE64(nonce >> BL)); the chain form takes the host's (fg3_pseed)
#if IH_FG2_CHAIN
    st4[2] = make_uint4(fg3_pseed[0], fg3_pseed[1], fg3_pseed[2], fg3_pseed[3]); st4[3] = make_uint4(fg3_pseed[4], fg3_pseed[5], fg3_pseed[6], fg3_pseed[7]);
#else
    {
        uint32_t qn = fg_tag(buf, 3);
        for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) buf[qn + i] = Eb[i]; qn += 32;
        for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) buf[qn + i] = prefix[64 + i]; qn += 32;
        const uint64_t bn = nonce >> (uint32_t)IH_FG2_BL;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) buf[qn + i] = (uint8_t)(bn >> (8 * i)); qn += 8;
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, qn, 0, o);
        st4[2] = make_uint4(o[0], o[1], o[2], o[3]); st4[3] = make_uint4(o[4], o[5], o[6], o[7]);
    }
#endif
    // the sequence and the hash's words (under pair_log2 the sequence from the pair's seed BLAKE3(LP(sequence) || E || header || LE64(nonce >> pair_log2)), the words the hash's)
    {
        uint32_t sw = 0, sq[FG3_SEQW4] = {0}, hwv[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
#if IH_FG2_PAIR
        uint32_t yp[8];
        {
            uint32_t qn = fg_tag(buf, 5);
            for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) buf[qn + i] = Eb[i]; qn += 32;
            for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) buf[qn + i] = prefix[64 + i]; qn += 32;
            const uint64_t pn2 = nonce >> (uint32_t)IH_FG2_PAIR;
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) buf[qn + i] = (uint8_t)(pn2 >> (8 * i)); qn += 8;
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, qn, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) yp[k] = o[k];
        }
#endif
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ; ++i) {
            y[i & 7u] = fg_fold(y[i & 7u], i);
#if IH_FG2_BASE
            {   // version 24: the period's fixed order, the predicates the shape's
                const uint32_t eb = fg3_base_seq[i];
                sw |= (eb & 255u) << (8u * (i & 3u)); if ((i & 3u) == 3u || i + 1u == (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ) { sq[i >> 2] = sw; sw = 0; }
                hwv[i & 3u] = fg3_base_hw[i];
                if ((i & 3u) == 3u || i + 1u == (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ) st4[(FG3_OFF_HW >> 2) + (i >> 2)] = make_uint4(hwv[0], hwv[1], hwv[2], hwv[3]);
                continue;
            }
#endif
#if IH_FG2_PAIR
            yp[i & 7u] = fg_fold(yp[i & 7u], i); const uint32_t e = yp[i & 7u] % (uint32_t)IH_FG_WORDS;
#else
            const uint32_t e = y[i & 7u] % (uint32_t)IH_FG_WORDS;
#endif
            sw |= (e & 255u) << (8u * (i & 3u)); if ((i & 3u) == 3u || i + 1u == (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ) { sq[i >> 2] = sw; sw = 0; }
            y[(i + 4u) & 7u] = fg_fold(y[(i + 4u) & 7u], 4096u + i); hwv[i & 3u] = y[(i + 4u) & 7u];
            if ((i & 3u) == 3u || i + 1u == (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ) st4[(FG3_OFF_HW >> 2) + (i >> 2)] = make_uint4(hwv[0], hwv[1], hwv[2], hwv[3]);
        }
#pragma unroll
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < FG3_SEQW4 / 4u; ++i) st4[4u + i] = make_uint4(sq[4u * i], sq[4u * i + 1u], sq[4u * i + 2u], sq[4u * i + 3u]);
    }
    // the init chain: R (16 lanes x 32), then SP (16 x SPW), then WN (16 x 16) under fp_mode 6
    {
        uint32_t in_ = fg_tag(buf, 1);
        for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) buf[in_ + i] = Eb[i]; in_ += 32;
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[in_ + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[in_ + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[in_ + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[in_ + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); } in_ += 32;
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, in_, 0, o);
        uint32_t xi[8];
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) xi[k] = o[k];
#if IH_FG3_INIT8
        // the chain's eight seed words to the state: the search kernel's lanes run the eight independent fold chains (word nc on chain nc & 7)
        st4[FG3_OFF_R >> 2] = make_uint4(xi[0], xi[1], xi[2], xi[3]); st4[(FG3_OFF_R >> 2) + 1] = make_uint4(xi[4], xi[5], xi[6], xi[7]);
#else
        // the chain in its own order (lane-major) straight into the state as uint4s: R, then SP, then WN
        const uint32_t total = 512u + 16u * FG3_SPW + 16u * FG3_WNW;
        uint32_t q[4];
        for (uint32_t nc = 0; nc < total; ++nc) { xi[nc & 7u] = fg_fold(xi[nc & 7u], nc); q[nc & 3u] = xi[nc & 7u]; if ((nc & 3u) == 3u) st4[(FG3_OFF_R >> 2) + (nc >> 2)] = make_uint4(q[0], q[1], q[2], q[3]); }
#endif
    }
}
// one warp a block (32 threads): the attempt index is blockIdx.x's, a warp-uniform value, so the entry dispatch is a uniform
// switch and ptxas emits the bare SHFL (a per-lane index makes every shuffle a WARPSYNC collective)
#ifndef IH_FG3_MINB
#define IH_FG3_MINB 1   // the resident warps an SM the compiler allocates registers for (tune minb)
#endif
extern "C" __global__ void __launch_bounds__(32, IH_FG3_MINB)
ih_search(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint4* __restrict__ dataset, uint32_t* __restrict__ state, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params) {
    (void)start_nonce; (void)params;
    const uint32_t warp_id = blockIdx.x, n_warps = gridDim.x;
    const uint32_t lane = threadIdx.x & 31u;
    // the lane's scratchpad fg3_sp[FG3_SPW][IH_BLOCK] is declared at file scope by the generated text (fp_mode 3 and 4)
#ifndef IH_FG3_INIT8
#define IH_FG3_INIT8 0
#endif
#ifndef IH_FG3_DIVLANE
#define IH_FG3_DIVLANE 0
#endif
#ifndef IH_FG3_CSEL
#define IH_FG3_CSEL 0
#endif
#ifndef IH_FG2_BASE
#define IH_FG2_BASE 0
#endif
#ifndef IH_FG3_SP8
#define IH_FG3_SP8 0
#endif
#if IH_FG3_INIT8 && IH_FG3_SP8
    uint32_t* fg3_init_sh[2] = { (uint32_t*)fg3_sp8[0], (uint32_t*)fg3_sp8[1] };   // version 25: the init chain's window aliases the scratchpad (seeded after R and WN)
#elif IH_FG3_INIT8
    uint32_t* fg3_init_sh[2] = { (uint32_t*)fg3_pfb[0], (uint32_t*)fg3_pfb[1] };   // the init chain's 192-word window a half aliases the prefetch ring (one warp a block)
#endif
#if IH_FG3_APW == 2
    const uint32_t l = lane & 15u, half = lane >> 4; const bool writer = true; const uint32_t wmask = 0xFFFFu << (16u * half);   // each half its own member mask
#else
    const uint32_t l = lane & 15u, half = 0u; const bool writer = lane < 16u; const uint32_t wmask = 0xFFFFFFFFu;   // lanes 16..31 run the attempt in duplicate and write nothing
#endif
    const uint32_t PERWORD = (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS / (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ;
#if IH_FG3_SP8
    const uint32_t spb = (uint32_t)__cvta_generic_to_shared(&fg3_sp8[half][0]);   // version 25: the half's scratchpad
#endif
#if IH_FG3_APW == 2 && IH_FG2_PAIR
    // the pair form: the warp's two attempts are the nonces of one pair (nonce >> pair_log2 equal), so they share the sequence
    // and the entry's switch reads it from the pair's lower attempt (a warp-uniform address: the bare shuffles stay)
    const uint32_t first = (uint32_t)(start_nonce & 1u);
    for (uint32_t g = warp_id; 2u * g < count + first; g += n_warps) {
        const uint32_t giL = 2u * g - first;   // wraps to the top under first = 1 at g = 0: that half is inactive
        const uint32_t gi = giL + half; const bool active = gi < count;
        const uint32_t giu = (giL < count) ? giL : giL + 1u;   // the pair's active attempt, the sequence's source
        uint32_t* st = state + (size_t)(active ? gi : giu) * FG3_STATE_WORDS;
        const uint32_t* stu = state + (size_t)giu * FG3_STATE_WORDS;
#else
    for (uint32_t g = warp_id; g * (uint32_t)IH_FG3_APW < count; g += n_warps) {
        const uint32_t gi = g * (uint32_t)IH_FG3_APW + half; const bool active = gi < count;
        uint32_t* st = state + (size_t)(active ? gi : count - 1u) * FG3_STATE_WORDS;   // a half past the count runs the last attempt's state in lockstep and writes nothing
        const uint32_t* stu = st;
#endif
        uint32_t R[32]; uint32_t WN[16];
#if IH_FG3_INIT8
        // the init chain on eight lanes a half: chain j = lane & 7 folds the words nc = j, j + 8, ... from its seed word; four passes of
        // 192 words through the half's shared window; a lane's 32 registers lie in one pass (192 = 6 x 32), its 16 window words too
        {
            const uint32_t j = l & 7u; uint32_t x = st[FG3_OFF_R + j];
            const uint32_t total = 512u + 16u * FG3_SPW + 16u * FG3_WNW;
            for (uint32_t pass = 0; pass < 4u; ++pass) {
                const uint32_t base = 192u * pass;
                if (l < 8u) { for (uint32_t m = 0; m < 24u; ++m) { const uint32_t nc = base + j + 8u * m; if (nc < total) { x = fg_fold(x, nc); fg3_init_sh[half][nc - base] = x; } } }
                __syncwarp(wmask);
                if (l / 6u == pass) {
#pragma unroll
                    for (int r = 0; r < 32; ++r) R[r] = fg3_init_sh[half][32u * l - base + (uint32_t)r];
                }
#if FG3_SPW
                if ((512u + 16u * l) / 192u == pass) {
#pragma unroll
                    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < FG3_SPW; ++i) fg3_sp[i][threadIdx.x] = fg3_init_sh[half][512u + 16u * l - base + i];
                }
#endif
#if FG3_WNW
                if ((512u + 16u * l) / 192u == pass) {
#pragma unroll
                    for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) WN[i] = fg3_init_sh[half][512u + 16u * l - base + (uint32_t)i];
                }
#else
#pragma unroll
                for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) WN[i] = 0u;
#endif
                __syncwarp(wmask);
            }
#if IH_FG3_SP8
            // version 25: the chain's words from 768 seed the scratchpad (word i = 8 m + j on chain j, the lane's own stream), each at its cell's byte
#ifndef IH_FG3_NOSEED
#define IH_FG3_NOSEED 0
#endif
            if (!IH_FG3_NOSEED && l < 8u) { for (uint32_t m = 0; m < (uint32_t)IH_FG3_SP8 / 32u; ++m) { const uint32_t i = 8u * m + j; x = fg_fold(x, total + i); fg3_sts32(spb + fg3_sp8_phys(i >> 1) + 4u * (j & 1u), x); } }   // noseed=1: a figure experiment (inexact), the seeding's cost by difference
            __syncwarp(wmask);
#endif
        }
#else
        const uint4* r4 = (const uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_R + 32u * l);
#pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < 8; ++r) { const uint4 v = r4[r]; R[4 * r] = v.x; R[4 * r + 1] = v.y; R[4 * r + 2] = v.z; R[4 * r + 3] = v.w; }
#if FG3_SPW
        { const uint4* s4 = (const uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_SP + FG3_SPW * l);
#pragma unroll
          for (uint32_t i = 0; i < FG3_SPW / 4u; ++i) { const uint4 v = s4[i]; fg3_sp[4 * i][threadIdx.x] = v.x; fg3_sp[4 * i + 1][threadIdx.x] = v.y; fg3_sp[4 * i + 2][threadIdx.x] = v.z; fg3_sp[4 * i + 3][threadIdx.x] = v.w; } }
#endif
#if FG3_WNW
        { const uint4* w4 = (const uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_WN + 16u * l);
#pragma unroll
          for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) { const uint4 v = w4[i]; WN[4 * i] = v.x; WN[4 * i + 1] = v.y; WN[4 * i + 2] = v.z; WN[4 * i + 3] = v.w; } }
#else
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) WN[i] = 0u;
#endif
#endif   // IH_FG3_INIT8
        uint32_t seqw[(IH_FG2_SEQ + 3) / 4];
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < (IH_FG2_SEQ + 3) / 4; ++i) seqw[i] = stu[FG3_OFF_SEQ + (uint32_t)i];
#ifndef IH_FG_SKIP
#define IH_FG_SKIP 0
#endif
        fg3_kx kx; fg3_prologue(R, l, dataset, wmask, kx, half);
#if !IH_FG3_CSEL
        const uint32_t xsel_ref = 0u;
#endif
#if IH_FG3_CSEL
        uint32_t xsel = fg_fold(stu[FG3_OFF_Z + 7u], 65536u);   // chain_select: the first entry's read word from the seed, then the previous entry's final lane state
        uint32_t wsel = (seqw[0] & 255u);
#define xsel_ref xsel
#endif
#if IH_FG3_SP8
        if (!IH_FG_SKIP) fg3_run25(R, WN, seqw[0], seqw[1], st + FG3_OFF_HW, l, dataset, wmask, kx, half, spb);
#endif
        for (uint32_t ei = 0; ei < ((IH_FG_SKIP || IH_FG3_SP8) ? 0u : (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ); ++ei) {
            const uint32_t hword = st[FG3_OFF_HW + ei];
#if IH_FG3_DIVLANE
            const uint32_t wi = fg_fold(hword, 4096u + l) % (uint32_t)IH_FG_WORDS;   // the screen's per-thread interpreter (a figure, inexact by construction): every lane its own programme, the switch 16-way divergent
#elif IH_FG3_CSEL
            const uint32_t wi = wsel;
#else
            const uint32_t wi = (seqw[ei >> 2] >> (8u * (ei & 3u))) & 255u;
#endif
#if IH_FG3_KEYED_ENTRY
            fg3_keyed_entry(R, l, ei * PERWORD, dataset, wmask, kx, 0, half, xsel_ref, st + FG3_OFF_KS, st + FG3_OFF_S, params + 16u);   // the entry's first read (the keyed read when the document's S keys it): one branch an entry; the shares into st, the secret's words from params (a half past the count or a duplicate upper half runs the written attempt's own walk and stores the same bytes to the same words)
#elif IH_FG3_HOIST
            if (((ei * PERWORD) % (uint32_t)IH_FG_FOLD) == 0u) fg3_keyed(R, l, ei * PERWORD, dataset, wmask, kx, 0, half, xsel_ref);   // the keyed read at the entry's first step
#endif
#if IH_FG3_KEYED
            fg3_dispatch(wi, R, threadIdx.x, WN, hword, ei * PERWORD, l, dataset, wmask, kx, half, st + FG3_OFF_Z, xsel_ref, st + FG3_OFF_KS, st + FG3_OFF_S, params + 16u);
#else
            fg3_dispatch(wi, R, threadIdx.x, WN, hword, ei * PERWORD, l, dataset, wmask, kx, half, st + FG3_OFF_Z, xsel_ref);
#endif
#if IH_FG3_CSEL
            {   // the entry's end: the fold over the 16 lanes' r0 and r1 (the state movement the chip is charged for), the next entry's shape and reads
                uint32_t x = xsel;
#pragma unroll
                for (int q = 0; q < 16; ++q) { const uint32_t r0 = fg3_shfl(R[0], (uint32_t)q, wmask), r1 = fg3_shfl(R[1], (uint32_t)q, wmask); x = (x ^ r0) + r1; }
                xsel = fg_fold(x, 1u + ei); wsel = xsel % (uint32_t)IH_FG_WORDS;
                if (ei + 1u < (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ) { const uint32_t sh = 8u * ((ei + 1u) & 3u); seqw[(ei + 1u) >> 2] = (seqw[(ei + 1u) >> 2] & ~(255u << sh)) | (wsel << sh); }   // the chained sequence is the digest's
            }
#endif
        }
#if IH_FG3_CSEL
        if (active && writer && l == 0u) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < (IH_FG2_SEQ + 3) / 4; ++i) st[FG3_OFF_SEQ + (uint32_t)i] = seqw[i];
        }
#endif
#if IH_FG2_LEAN
        if (active && writer) {
            // the lean mix: the lane's words (its registers, then its scratchpad words, then its window words) into a 16-byte accumulator
            uint32_t acc[4] = { 0x9E3779B9u ^ l, 0x85EBCA6Bu, 0xC2B2AE35u, 0x27D4EB2Fu };
#pragma unroll
            for (int r = 0; r < 32; ++r) acc[r & 3] = (acc[r & 3] ^ R[r]) + R[r];
#if FG3_SPW
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < FG3_SPW; ++i) { const uint32_t w = fg3_sp[i][threadIdx.x]; acc[i & 3u] = (acc[i & 3u] ^ w) + w; }
#endif
#if FG3_WNW
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) acc[i & 3] = (acc[i & 3] ^ WN[i]) + WN[i];
#endif
#if IH_FG3_SP8
            // version 25: the lane's slice of the scratchpad (cells 0 .. W-1 on the lane's bank pair), word by word in the reference's order
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t w = 0; w < (uint32_t)IH_FG3_SP8_W; ++w) { const uint2 v = fg3_lds64(spb + (w << 7) + (l << 3)); acc[(2u * w) & 3u] = (acc[(2u * w) & 3u] ^ v.x) + v.x; acc[(2u * w + 1u) & 3u] = (acc[(2u * w + 1u) & 3u] ^ v.y) + v.y; }
#endif
            *(uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_R + 4u * l) = make_uint4(acc[0], acc[1], acc[2], acc[3]);
#if IH_FG3_SP8 && IH_FG3_DEBUG
            *(uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_WN + 16u * l) = make_uint4(R[0], R[1], R[2], R[3]); *(uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_WN + 16u * l + 4u) = make_uint4(WN[0], WN[1], fg3_lds64(spb + (l << 3)).x, fg3_lds64(spb + (l << 3)).y);   // the debugging form: the lane's first registers, window words and slice cell beside its accumulator
#endif
        }
#else
        if (active && writer) {
            uint4* w4 = (uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_R + 32u * l);
#pragma unroll
            for (int r = 0; r < 8; ++r) w4[r] = make_uint4(R[4 * r], R[4 * r + 1], R[4 * r + 2], R[4 * r + 3]);
#if FG3_SPW
            uint4* s4 = (uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_SP + FG3_SPW * l);
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < FG3_SPW / 4u; ++i) s4[i] = make_uint4(fg3_sp[4 * i][threadIdx.x], fg3_sp[4 * i + 1][threadIdx.x], fg3_sp[4 * i + 2][threadIdx.x], fg3_sp[4 * i + 3][threadIdx.x]);
#endif
#if FG3_WNW
            uint4* n4 = (uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_WN + 16u * l);
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) n4[i] = make_uint4(WN[4 * i], WN[4 * i + 1], WN[4 * i + 2], WN[4 * i + 3]);
#endif
        }
#endif   // IH_FG2_LEAN
    }
}
// the mix2 message's word stream past the 22-byte tag: E | pseed | seq | R[lane][reg] | SP[lane][word] | WN[lane][word]
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t fg3_vword(uint32_t q, const uint32_t* __restrict__ st, const uint32_t* __restrict__ Ew) {
    if (q >= FG3_V_WORDS) return 0u;
    if (q < 8u) return Ew[q];
    if (q < FG3_V_R) return st[q];   // pseed then the sequence's words, as laid out
    if (q < FG3_V_KS) return st[q - FG3_V_R + FG3_OFF_R];   // R, SP and WN lie in the stream's own order from FG3_OFF_R
    return st[q - FG3_V_KS + FG3_OFF_KS];   // the shares whole, in index order (F-504 (i)+)
}
#if IH_FG3_FPMODE == 6 && !IH_FG3_CSEL
// THE REGROUPING FORM (the screen's last cell, main's order 09:2x UK 15 Oct 2026): one compiled entry a launch; before it every attempt
// appends its id to its next shape's queue (ih_fg3_queue: one atomic a attempt), then full warps run each shape's compiled body from
// the queues in order (ih_fg3_entry), the state (32 registers, 16 window words and the saved prefetch word a lane) reloaded from and
// saved to global memory at every boundary; every attempt kept, no selection. The pipeline (P) is off under this form (the reads in
// flight do not cross a launch): tune pf=0.
extern "C" __global__ void __launch_bounds__(128)
ih_fg3_queue(uint32_t count, uint32_t ei, const uint32_t* __restrict__ state, uint32_t* __restrict__ queues, uint32_t* __restrict__ counts) {
    const uint32_t gi = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (gi >= count) return;
    const uint32_t* st = state + (size_t)gi * FG3_STATE_WORDS;
    const uint32_t wi = (st[FG3_OFF_SEQ + (ei >> 2)] >> (8u * (ei & 3u))) & 255u;   // the attempt's next shape (its own sequence)
    const uint32_t pos = atomicAdd(&counts[wi], 1u);
    queues[(size_t)wi * count + pos] = gi;
}
extern "C" __global__ void __launch_bounds__(32, IH_FG3_MINB)
ih_fg3_entry(uint32_t count, uint32_t ei, const uint4* __restrict__ dataset, uint32_t* __restrict__ state, const uint32_t* __restrict__ queues, const uint32_t* __restrict__ counts) {
    const uint32_t warp_id = blockIdx.x, n_warps = gridDim.x;
    const uint32_t lane = threadIdx.x & 31u;
#if IH_FG3_APW == 2
    const uint32_t l = lane & 15u, half = lane >> 4; const uint32_t wmask = 0xFFFFu << (16u * half);
#else
    const uint32_t l = lane & 15u, half = 0u; const uint32_t wmask = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
#endif
    const uint32_t PERWORD = (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS / (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ;
    // the prefix over the 16 queues (uniform): the warp's slots in the concatenation
    uint32_t pre[IH_FG_WORDS + 1]; pre[0] = 0u;
#pragma unroll
    for (int q = 0; q < IH_FG_WORDS; ++q) pre[q + 1] = pre[q] + counts[q];
    for (uint32_t g = warp_id; g * (uint32_t)IH_FG3_APW < count; g += n_warps) {
        const uint32_t slot = g * (uint32_t)IH_FG3_APW + half; const bool active = slot < count;
        uint32_t wi = 0u, pos = active ? slot : count - 1u;
#pragma unroll
        for (int q = 0; q < IH_FG_WORDS; ++q) if (pos >= pre[q + 1]) wi = (uint32_t)q + 1u;
        const uint32_t gi = queues[(size_t)wi * count + (pos - pre[wi])];
        uint32_t* st = state + (size_t)gi * FG3_STATE_WORDS;
        // the state reloaded (the charge: 49 words a lane a boundary)
        uint32_t R[32]; uint32_t WN[16];
        { const uint4* r4 = (const uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_R + 32u * l);
#pragma unroll
          for (int r = 0; r < 8; ++r) { const uint4 v = r4[r]; R[4 * r] = v.x; R[4 * r + 1] = v.y; R[4 * r + 2] = v.z; R[4 * r + 3] = v.w; } }
        { const uint4* w4 = (const uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_WN + 16u * l);
#pragma unroll
          for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) { const uint4 v = w4[i]; WN[4 * i] = v.x; WN[4 * i + 1] = v.y; WN[4 * i + 2] = v.z; WN[4 * i + 3] = v.w; } }
        fg3_kx kx; kx.h = ei ? st[FG3_OFF_KX + l] : R[0];
        const uint32_t hword = st[FG3_OFF_HW + ei];
#if IH_FG3_KEYED
        fg3_dispatch(wi, R, threadIdx.x, WN, hword, ei * PERWORD, l, dataset, wmask, kx, half, st + FG3_OFF_Z, 0u, (uint32_t*)0, (const uint32_t*)0, (const uint32_t*)0);   // the regrouping form is refused under the keyed document (the engine); the text compiles
#else
        fg3_dispatch(wi, R, threadIdx.x, WN, hword, ei * PERWORD, l, dataset, wmask, kx, half, st + FG3_OFF_Z, 0u);
#endif
        if (active) {   // the state saved (49 words a lane)
            uint4* w4 = (uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_R + 32u * l);
#pragma unroll
            for (int r = 0; r < 8; ++r) w4[r] = make_uint4(R[4 * r], R[4 * r + 1], R[4 * r + 2], R[4 * r + 3]);
            uint4* n4 = (uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_WN + 16u * l);
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) n4[i] = make_uint4(WN[4 * i], WN[4 * i + 1], WN[4 * i + 2], WN[4 * i + 3]);
            st[FG3_OFF_KX + l] = kx.h;
        }
    }
}
// the lean accumulators from the saved state (the regrouping form's last step; the search kernel folds them in registers)
extern "C" __global__ void __launch_bounds__(128)
ih_fg3_acc(uint32_t count, uint32_t* __restrict__ state) {
    const uint32_t tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x, gi = tid >> 4, l = tid & 15u;
    if (gi >= count) return;
    uint32_t* st = state + (size_t)gi * FG3_STATE_WORDS;
    uint32_t acc[4] = { 0x9E3779B9u ^ l, 0x85EBCA6Bu, 0xC2B2AE35u, 0x27D4EB2Fu };
    for (int r = 0; r < 32; ++r) { const uint32_t w = st[FG3_OFF_R + 32u * l + (uint32_t)r]; acc[r & 3] = (acc[r & 3] ^ w) + w; }
    for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) { const uint32_t w = st[FG3_OFF_WN + 16u * l + (uint32_t)i]; acc[i & 3] = (acc[i & 3] ^ w) + w; }
    __syncwarp();
    *(uint4*)(st + FG3_OFF_R + 4u * l) = make_uint4(acc[0], acc[1], acc[2], acc[3]);
}
#endif
extern "C" __global__ void __launch_bounds__(128)
ih_fg3_mix(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope, const uint32_t* __restrict__ state,
           uint32_t* __restrict__ results, uint32_t max_results) {
    const uint32_t tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const uint32_t gi = tid / FG3_G, c = tid % FG3_G;
    const uint32_t gmask = ((1u << FG3_G) - 1u) << ((threadIdx.x & 31u) & ~(FG3_G - 1u));
    if (gi >= count) return;
    const uint32_t* st = state + (size_t)gi * FG3_STATE_WORDS;
    const uint8_t* Eb = envelope + IH_PARAMS_LEN + 12;
    uint32_t Ew[8];
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) Ew[k] = (uint32_t)Eb[4 * k] | ((uint32_t)Eb[4 * k + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)Eb[4 * k + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)Eb[4 * k + 3] << 24);
#if (IH_FG2_SEQ % 4) != 0
    // the sequence's bytes break the word stream: one lane a group builds the message and streams it (343 bytes under mode 9's lean form)
    uint32_t M[8];
    if (c != 0u) return;
    {
        fg_b3s h; fg_b3s_init(h);
        uint8_t tb[32]; uint32_t tn = fg_tag(tb, 4);
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < tn; ++i) fg_b3s_byte(h, tb[i]);
        for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) fg_b3s_byte(h, Eb[i]);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) fg_b3s_word(h, st[FG3_OFF_PSEED + k]);
        for (int i = 0; i < IH_FG2_SEQ; ++i) fg_b3s_byte(h, (uint8_t)(st[FG3_OFF_SEQ + (i >> 2)] >> (8 * (i & 3))));
        for (uint32_t q = 0; q < FG3_LANE_BYTES / 4u; ++q) fg_b3s_word(h, st[FG3_OFF_R + q]);
        for (uint32_t q = 0; q < 8u * IH_FG3_KEYED; ++q) fg_b3s_word(h, st[FG3_OFF_KS + q]);   // the shares whole (F-504 (i)+)
        fg_b3s_final(h, M);
    }
#else
    // this lane's chunk
    uint32_t cv[8];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
    if (c < FG3_CHUNKS) {
        const uint32_t cbeg = c * 1024u, cend = (cbeg + 1024u < FG3_MSG_BYTES) ? cbeg + 1024u : FG3_MSG_BYTES;
        const uint32_t nblocks = (cend - cbeg + 63u) / 64u;
        for (uint32_t b = 0; b < nblocks; ++b) {
            const uint32_t o = cbeg + 64u * b;
            const uint32_t blen = (cend - o < 64u) ? cend - o : 64u;
            uint32_t m[16];
            if (o == 0u) {
                // the first block byte by byte: tag(21 + 1) || E || the first 10 bytes of pseed
                uint8_t tb[64]; uint32_t tn = fg_tag(tb, 4);
                for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) tb[tn + i] = Eb[i]; tn += 32;
                for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 10u; ++i) tb[tn + i] = (uint8_t)(st[FG3_OFF_PSEED + (i >> 2)] >> (8u * (i & 3u)));
#pragma unroll
                for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) m[i] = (uint32_t)tb[4 * i] | ((uint32_t)tb[4 * i + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)tb[4 * i + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)tb[4 * i + 3] << 24);
            } else {
                const uint32_t q0 = (o - 22u) >> 2;   // (o - 22) & 3 == 2 for every block past the first
                uint32_t vv[17];
                if (q0 >= FG3_V_R + 3u && q0 + 17u <= FG3_V_KS) {   // inside R, SP and WN (the shares past FG3_V_KS go word by word below): a 16-byte aligned window of five uint4s, the 17 words from its aligned offset
                    const uint32_t base = q0 - FG3_V_R + FG3_OFF_R, al = base & 3u;
                    const uint4* p4 = (const uint4*)(st + base - al);
                    uint32_t ww[20];
#pragma unroll
                    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) { const uint4 v = p4[i]; ww[4 * i] = v.x; ww[4 * i + 1] = v.y; ww[4 * i + 2] = v.z; ww[4 * i + 3] = v.w; }
#pragma unroll
                    for (int i = 0; i < 17; ++i) vv[i] = ww[i + al];
                } else {
#pragma unroll
                    for (int i = 0; i < 17; ++i) vv[i] = fg3_vword(q0 + (uint32_t)i, st, Ew);
                }
#pragma unroll
                for (int w = 0; w < 16; ++w) m[w] = __funnelshift_r(vv[w], vv[w + 1], 16);
                if (blen < 64u) {
#pragma unroll
                    for (int w = 0; w < 16; ++w) { const uint32_t lo = 4u * (uint32_t)w; m[w] = (lo >= blen) ? 0u : (blen - lo >= 4u ? m[w] : (m[w] & ((1u << (8u * (blen - lo))) - 1u))); }
                }
            }
            const uint32_t flags = (b == 0u ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u) | (b + 1u == nblocks ? B3_CHUNK_END : 0u) | ((FG3_CHUNKS == 1u && b + 1u == nblocks) ? B3_ROOT : 0u);
            uint32_t out[16];
            b3_compress_ctr(cv, m, c, blen, flags, out);
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = out[i];
        }
    }
    // every lane of the group gathers the group's chunk values (the shuffles before any lane leaves), then lane 0 carries on
    uint32_t cvs[FG3_CHUNKS][8];
    for (uint32_t ch = 0; ch < FG3_CHUNKS; ++ch) {
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cvs[ch][i] = __shfl_sync(gmask, cv[i], ((threadIdx.x & 31u) & ~(FG3_G - 1u)) + ch);
    }
    if (c != 0u) return;
    // the parent tree on lane 0 of the group (the standard chaining-value stack over the chunks in order)
    uint32_t M[8];
    if (FG3_CHUNKS == 1u) {
#pragma unroll
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) M[i] = cvs[0][i];
    } else {
        uint32_t stack[5][8]; uint32_t stack_len = 0;
        for (uint32_t ch = 0; ch < FG3_CHUNKS; ++ch) {
            uint32_t ncv[8];
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) ncv[i] = cvs[ch][i];
            if (ch + 1u == FG3_CHUNKS) {
                uint32_t rem = stack_len;
                while (rem > 0u) { uint32_t t[8]; fg_b3s_parent(stack[rem - 1], ncv, rem == 1u ? B3_ROOT : 0u, t);
#pragma unroll
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) ncv[i] = t[i]; --rem; }
#pragma unroll
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) M[i] = ncv[i];
            } else {
                uint64_t total = ch + 1u;
                while ((total & 1u) == 0u) { uint32_t t[8]; fg_b3s_parent(stack[stack_len - 1], ncv, 0u, t); --stack_len;
#pragma unroll
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) ncv[i] = t[i]; total >>= 1; }
#pragma unroll
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) stack[stack_len][i] = ncv[i];
                ++stack_len;
            }
        }
    }
