#endif   // the word stream or the byte path
    // the common final digest F over S and M, and the target compare
    uint8_t prefix[96]; fg3_prefix(params, envelope, prefix);
    uint8_t buf[160]; uint32_t o[16];
    uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
    for (int i = 0; i < 44; ++i) buf[fn + i] = prefix[20 + i];
    fn += 44;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { const uint32_t s = st[FG3_OFF_S + k]; buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)s; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(s >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(s >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(s >> 24); }
    fn += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
    fn += 32;
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
    bool le = true;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { const uint32_t av = bswap32(o[i]); const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i]; if (av < bv) { le = true; break; } if (av > bv) { le = false; break; } }
    if (le) {
        const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
        if (rs < max_results) {
            const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
            uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
            e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = o[i];
        }
    }
}
#else
extern "C" __global__ void IH_SEARCH_BOUNDS
ih_search(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint4* __restrict__ dataset, uint32_t items_unused,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ scratch, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ results, uint32_t max_results, unsigned long long tex_unused) {
    (void)scratch; (void)items_unused; (void)tex_unused;
    const uint32_t tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const uint32_t nthreads = gridDim.x * blockDim.x;
    const uint32_t warp_id = tid >> 5, n_warps = nthreads >> 5;
    const uint32_t lane = threadIdx.x & 31u;
    const uint32_t warp_in_block = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    __shared__ uint32_t fg_sh[IH_BLOCK / 32][1 + IH_FG2_APW2][16][32 + IH_FG2_SPW];
#if IH_FG2_SPSHARED
    __shared__ uint32_t fg_sp[IH_BLOCK][IH_FG2_SPW];
#endif
    // the common prefix (R0A seed domain): tag(0) || header[12] || BLAKE3(params) || params[0..8]; the 32 header bytes lie at prefix[64..96)
    const uint8_t* Eb = envelope + IH_PARAMS_LEN + 12;   // the epoch seed E (32 bytes), uploaded at prepare
    uint8_t prefix[96];
    uint32_t o[16];
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, envelope, IH_PARAMS_LEN, 0, o);
    uint32_t n = ih_tag(prefix, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) prefix[n + i] = envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + i];
    n += 12;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)o[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { const uint32_t w = params[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)w; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
    n += 32;
#if IH_FG2_APW2
    const uint32_t l = lane & 15u, half = lane >> 4;   // this program lane and this attempt's half of the warp
#else
    if (lane >= 16u) return;   // lanes 16..31 idle; lanes 0..15 are the program lanes
    const uint32_t l = lane; const uint32_t half = 0u;   // this program lane
#endif
#ifndef IH_FG_SKIP
#define IH_FG_SKIP 0
#endif
    const uint32_t STEPS = IH_FG_SKIP ? 0u : (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS, WORDS = (uint32_t)IH_FG_WORDS, FOLD = (uint32_t)IH_FG_FOLD;   // fgskip=1: a debugging form, the step loop skipped (the digest over the init state)
#if IH_FORGE2
    const uint32_t PERWORD = (uint32_t)IH_ROUNDS / (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ;   // the steps an entry of the sequence runs
#else
    const uint32_t PERWORD = STEPS / WORDS;                  // the steps a word runs
#endif
#if IH_FG2_APW2
    for (uint32_t g = warp_id; g * 2u < count; g += n_warps) {   // the warp's pair; a half past the count still runs in lockstep and writes nothing
        const uint32_t gi = g * 2u + half; const bool active = gi < count;
#else
    for (uint32_t gi = warp_id; gi < count; gi += n_warps) {
        const bool active = true;
#endif
        const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
        uint32_t S[8], M[8], F[8];
        uint8_t buf[160];
        for (int i = 0; i < 96; ++i) buf[i] = prefix[i];
        put_u64be(buf + 96, nonce);
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, 104, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
        // --- the hash's seed = BLAKE3(LP(program) || E || S) ---
        uint8_t pb[96]; uint32_t pn = fg_tag(pb, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) pb[pn + i] = Eb[i]; pn += 32;
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { pb[pn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; pb[pn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); pb[pn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); pb[pn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); } pn += 32;
        uint32_t seedw[8]; b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, pb, pn, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) seedw[k] = o[k];
        uint32_t W0[IH_FG_WORDS], W1[IH_FG_WORDS], W2[IH_FG_WORDS], W3[IH_FG_WORDS];
#if IH_FORGE2
        // --- the period: pseed = BLAKE3(LP(period) || E || header || LE64(nonce >> BL)); the batch's shapes by the fold chains ---
        uint32_t pseed[8];
        {
            uint8_t qb[128]; uint32_t qn = fg_tag(qb, 3);
            for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) qb[qn + i] = Eb[i]; qn += 32;
            for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) qb[qn + i] = prefix[64 + i]; qn += 32;
            const uint64_t bn = nonce >> (uint32_t)IH_FG2_BL;
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) qb[qn + i] = (uint8_t)(bn >> (8 * i)); qn += 8;
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, qb, qn, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) pseed[k] = o[k];
#if !IH_FORGE2_COMPILED
            uint32_t xq[8];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) xq[k] = pseed[k];
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < WORDS; ++i) {
#pragma unroll
                for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) { const uint32_t idx = 4u * i + (uint32_t)j; xq[idx & 7u] = fg_fold(xq[idx & 7u], idx); const uint32_t wv = xq[idx & 7u];
                    if (j == 0) W0[i] = wv; else if (j == 1) W1[i] = wv; else if (j == 2) W2[i] = wv; else W3[i] = wv; }
            }
#else
            (void)W0; (void)W1; (void)W2; (void)W3;   // the compiled form: the shapes are baked into fg2_dispatch on the host from this same seed
#endif
        }
#if IH_FORGE2_COMPILED
        uint32_t Hh[32];   // the hash's words for the current entry (the scratchpad addresses and the cmov predicate), hoisted
#endif
        // --- the hash's sequence over the shapes and its own words ---
        uint8_t seq[IH_FG2_SEQ]; uint32_t hw[IH_FG2_SEQ];
        {
            uint32_t y[8];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) y[k] = seedw[k];
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < (uint32_t)IH_FG2_SEQ; ++i) { y[i & 7u] = fg_fold(y[i & 7u], i); seq[i] = (uint8_t)(y[i & 7u] % WORDS); y[(i + 4u) & 7u] = fg_fold(y[(i + 4u) & 7u], 4096u + i); hw[i] = y[(i + 4u) & 7u]; }
        }
#else
        // --- the programme: the hash's WORDS words by the fold chains over the seed, and its hash ---
        uint32_t phash[8];
        {
            uint32_t xq[8];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) xq[k] = seedw[k];
            uint8_t pbytes[IH_FG_WORDS * 16];
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < WORDS; ++i) {
#pragma unroll
                for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) { const uint32_t idx = 4u * i + (uint32_t)j; xq[idx & 7u] = fg_fold(xq[idx & 7u], idx); const uint32_t wv = xq[idx & 7u];
                    if (j == 0) W0[i] = wv; else if (j == 1) W1[i] = wv; else if (j == 2) W2[i] = wv; else W3[i] = wv;
                    pbytes[16u * i + 4u * j] = (uint8_t)wv; pbytes[16u * i + 4u * j + 1] = (uint8_t)(wv >> 8); pbytes[16u * i + 4u * j + 2] = (uint8_t)(wv >> 16); pbytes[16u * i + 4u * j + 3] = (uint8_t)(wv >> 24); }
            }
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, pbytes, WORDS * 16u, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) phash[k] = o[k];
        }
#endif
        // --- the state: R[32] and SP[256] from BLAKE3(LP(init) || E || S), each lane replaying the global fold chain ---
        uint32_t R[32];
#if IH_FG2_SPSHARED
        uint32_t* SP = fg_sp[threadIdx.x];
#else
        uint32_t SP[IH_FG2_SPW];
#endif
        {
            uint8_t ib[96]; uint32_t in_ = fg_tag(ib, 1);
            for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) ib[in_ + i] = Eb[i]; in_ += 32;
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { ib[in_ + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; ib[in_ + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); ib[in_ + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); ib[in_ + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); } in_ += 32;
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, ib, in_, 0, o);
            uint32_t xi[8];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) xi[k] = o[k];
            uint32_t nc = 0;
            for (uint32_t ll = 0; ll < 16u; ++ll)
                for (uint32_t r = 0; r < 32u; ++r, ++nc) { xi[nc & 7u] = fg_fold(xi[nc & 7u], nc);
#if IH_FORGE2 && IH_FG2_FPMODE == 2
                    const bool mk = r >= 24u;
#elif IH_FORGE2 && (IH_FG2_FPMODE == 1 || IH_FG2_FPMODE == 3)
                    const bool mk = false;
#else
                    const bool mk = r >= 20u;
#endif
                    const uint32_t v = mk ? fg_mask(xi[nc & 7u]) : xi[nc & 7u]; if (ll == l) R[r] = v; }
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 16u * IH_FG2_SPW; ++i, ++nc) { xi[nc & 7u] = fg_fold(xi[nc & 7u], nc); const uint32_t v = xi[nc & 7u]; if (i / IH_FG2_SPW == l) SP[i % IH_FG2_SPW] = v; }
        }
        // --- the interpreter ---
        const uint32_t* ds = (const uint32_t*)dataset;
        uint8_t cls[32];
        for (uint32_t t = 0; t < STEPS; ++t) {
            if ((t % FOLD) == 0u) {
                const uint32_t Lf = (t / FOLD) & 15u;
                const uint32_t r0 = __shfl_sync(FG_WMASK, R[0], Lf, 16);
                const uint64_t idx = (uint64_t)r0 % (uint64_t)IH_N_ITEMS;
                const uint32_t off = (l - Lf) & 15u;
                if (off < 4u) {
#pragma unroll
                    for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) { const uint32_t w = ds[idx * 64u + 16u * off + (uint32_t)k]; R[k] = fg_fold(R[k], w); }
                }
            }
#if IH_FORGE2 && IH_FORGE2_COMPILED
            // the compiled form: the batch's shapes are baked into fg2_dispatch (the host generated them from the period seed); the
            // hash's words H[k] = fold(hword, k) are hoisted once an entry; one call runs the entry's shape for a step
            const uint32_t ei = t / PERWORD; const uint32_t wi = (uint32_t)seq[ei];
            if ((t % PERWORD) == 0u) {
                const uint32_t hword = hw[ei];
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) Hh[k] = fg_fold(hword, (uint32_t)k);
            }
            fg2_dispatch(wi, R, SP, Hh, l);
            continue;
#elif IH_FORGE2
            const uint32_t ei = t / PERWORD; const uint32_t wi = (uint32_t)seq[ei]; const uint32_t hword = hw[ei];
            if ((t % PERWORD) == 0u) {
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) cls[k] = (IH_FG2_FPMODE == 1 || IH_FG2_FPMODE == 3) ? c_fg_quota_int[k] : c_fg_quota[k];
                for (int i = 31; i > 0; --i) { const uint32_t j = fg_fold(W0[wi], (uint32_t)i) % (uint32_t)(i + 1); const uint8_t tmp = cls[i]; cls[i] = cls[j]; cls[j] = tmp; }   // the shape's class order, always drawn
            }
#else
            const uint32_t wi = t / PERWORD;
            if ((t % PERWORD) == 0u) {
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) cls[k] = c_fg_quota[k];
#if IH_FG_DRAWN
                for (int i = 31; i > 0; --i) { const uint32_t j = fg_fold(W0[wi], (uint32_t)i) % (uint32_t)(i + 1); const uint8_t tmp = cls[i]; cls[i] = cls[j]; cls[j] = tmp; }
#endif
            }
#endif
            const uint32_t w1 = W1[wi], w2 = W2[wi], w3 = W3[wi];
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) {
#if IH_FORGE2 && IH_FG2_FPMODE == 3
                uint32_t ak = ((uint32_t)k + 21u) & 31u, bk = ((uint32_t)k + 9u) & 31u, dk = (uint32_t)k;
#else
                uint32_t ak = (7u * (uint32_t)k + 1u) & 31u, bk = (11u * (uint32_t)k + 5u) & 31u, dk = (uint32_t)k;
#endif
                const uint32_t imm = fg_fold(w1, (uint32_t)k), q = fg_fold(w2, (uint32_t)k);
#if IH_FORGE2 && !IH_FORGE2_COMPILED
                const uint32_t aimm = fg_fold(hword, (uint32_t)k);   // the hash's own word: the scratchpad addresses and the cmov predicate
#else
                const uint32_t aimm = imm;
#endif
                bool load = ((w3 >> k) & 1u) != 0u;
                const uint8_t C = cls[k];
#if IH_FORGE2 && IH_FG2_FPMODE == 2
                {   // the producer-masked wiring: the FP slots on the bank r24..r31 (no scratchpad load), the integer slots on r0..r23
                    const bool fp = (C == FGFMA || C == FGFADDSUB || C == FGFMUL || C == FGI2F || C == FGF2I);
                    if (fp) { load = false; if (C == FGI2F) { ak = ak % 24u; bk = bk % 24u; dk = 24u + ((uint32_t)k & 7u); } else if (C == FGF2I) { ak = 24u + (ak & 7u); bk = 24u + (bk & 7u); dk = (uint32_t)k % 24u; } else { ak = 24u + (ak & 7u); bk = 24u + (bk & 7u); dk = 24u + ((uint32_t)k & 7u); } }
                    else { ak = ak % 24u; bk = bk % 24u; dk = (uint32_t)k % 24u; }
                }
#endif
                const uint32_t a = R[ak];
                const uint32_t b = load ? SP[((R[bk] + aimm) & IH_FG2_SPMASK) >> 2] : R[bk];
                const uint32_t d0 = R[dk];
                const uint32_t xt = ((uint32_t)l + 1u + (q % 15u)) & 15u;
                const uint32_t xa = __shfl_sync(FG_WMASK, R[ak], xt, 16);
                uint32_t d = d0; bool w = true; bool store = false; uint32_t st_addr = 0u, st_val = 0u;
                switch (C) {
#if IH_FORGE2 && IH_FG2_FPMODE == 2
                    case FGFMA: d = fg_mask(__float_as_uint(fmaf(__uint_as_float(a), __uint_as_float(b), __uint_as_float(d0)))); break;   // mode 2: the bank is masked at its producers
                    case FGFADDSUB: d = fg_mask(__float_as_uint((q & 1u) ? __uint_as_float(a) - __uint_as_float(b) : __uint_as_float(a) + __uint_as_float(b))); break;
                    case FGFMUL: d = fg_mask(__float_as_uint(__uint_as_float(a) * __uint_as_float(b))); break;
#else
                    case FGFMA: d = fg_mask(__float_as_uint(fmaf(__uint_as_float(fg_mask(a)), __uint_as_float(fg_mask(b)), __uint_as_float(fg_mask(d0))))); break;
                    case FGFADDSUB: d = fg_mask(__float_as_uint((q & 1u) ? __uint_as_float(fg_mask(a)) - __uint_as_float(fg_mask(b)) : __uint_as_float(fg_mask(a)) + __uint_as_float(fg_mask(b)))); break;
                    case FGFMUL: d = fg_mask(__float_as_uint(__uint_as_float(fg_mask(a)) * __uint_as_float(fg_mask(b)))); break;
#endif
                    case FGIALU: switch (q % 5u) { case 0: d = a + b + imm; break; case 1: d = a - b - imm; break; case 2: d = a ^ b ^ imm; break; case 3: d = (a & b) ^ imm; break; default: d = (a | b) ^ imm; break; } break;
                    case FGROT: { const uint32_t sh = (imm ^ b) & 31u; switch (q & 3u) { case 0: d = ih_rotl(a, sh); break; case 1: d = ih_rotl(a, (32u - sh) & 31u); break; case 2: d = (a << sh) + b; break; default: d = (a >> sh) + b; break; } } break;
                    case FGMUL: d = a * b + imm; break;
                    case FGMULHI: d = __umulhi(a, b) ^ imm; break;
                    case FGI2F: d = fg_mask(__float_as_uint((float)(int)(a & 0x00FFFFFFu))); break;
                    case FGF2I: d = fg_mask(a) ^ imm; break;
                    case FGXLANE: d = xa + imm; break;
                    case FGSTORE: w = false; store = true; st_addr = ((a + aimm) & IH_FG2_SPMASK) >> 2; st_val = b; break;
                    default: d = ((b >> (aimm & 31u)) & 1u) ? a : d0; break;   // CMOV
                }
                if (w) R[dk] = d;
                if (store) SP[st_addr] = st_val;
            }
        }
        // --- the mix: gather every lane's R and SP into shared, lane 0 streams BLAKE3 ---
#pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < 32; ++r) fg_sh[warp_in_block][half][l][r] = R[r];
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < IH_FG2_SPW; ++i) fg_sh[warp_in_block][half][l][32 + i] = SP[i];
        __syncwarp(FG_WMASK);
        if (l == 0u) {
            fg_b3s h; fg_b3s_init(h);
#if IH_FORGE2
            uint8_t tb[32]; uint32_t tn = fg_tag(tb, 4);
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < tn; ++i) fg_b3s_byte(h, tb[i]);
            for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) fg_b3s_byte(h, Eb[i]);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) fg_b3s_word(h, pseed[k]);
            for (int i = 0; i < IH_FG2_SEQ; ++i) fg_b3s_byte(h, seq[i]);
#else
            uint8_t tb[32]; uint32_t tn = fg_tag(tb, 2);
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < tn; ++i) fg_b3s_byte(h, tb[i]);
            for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i) fg_b3s_byte(h, Eb[i]);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) fg_b3s_word(h, phash[k]);
#endif
            for (int ll = 0; ll < 16; ++ll)
#pragma unroll
                for (int r = 0; r < 32; ++r) fg_b3s_word(h, fg_sh[warp_in_block][half][ll][r]);
            for (int ll = 0; ll < 16; ++ll)
                for (uint32_t i = 0; i < IH_FG2_SPW; ++i) fg_b3s_word(h, fg_sh[warp_in_block][half][ll][32 + i]);
            fg_b3s_final(h, M);
        }
        __syncwarp(FG_WMASK);   // the gather read out before the next attempt refills it
        if (l == 0u) {
            // --- the common final digest F over S and M, and the target compare (the texture form's framing) ---
            uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
            for (int i = 0; i < 44; ++i) buf[fn + i] = prefix[20 + i];
            fn += 44;
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
            fn += 32;
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
            fn += 32;
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
            bool le = true;
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]); const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i]; if (av < bv) { le = true; break; } if (av > bv) { le = false; break; } }
            if (le && active) {
                const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
                if (rs < max_results) {
                    uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                    e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
#endif   // IH_FG3
#elif IH_R16
// VERSION 15, the bandwidth-first class (pow/ref/src/igneumhash.cpp namespace r16, the hash lead's text of 13 Oct 2026 14:2x UK; GPU A's
// port): one attempt per octet, lane l holding its 32 registers (four banks of eight). Every iteration issues the 16 independent item
// reads from the entry state (the address of bank b slot q from lane (4b+q) mod 8's register 8b under the key word), each lane taking
// its 32 bytes of every item (words 8l .. 8l+8: the octet's eight lanes read one 256-byte transaction per item), folds the words into
// bank b under the key words, then runs the period's programme (compiled in as ih_r16_prog) on its own registers. The mix M is
// BLAKE3(LP(mix) || K || the 256 registers LE), 1,075 bytes as two chunks and a parent, hashed by lane 0; F the common final digest.
// The period key's words ride as IH_R16_K0..7 (the module is rebuilt per period as the R0D family's is).
__device__ __forceinline__ void ih_r16_prog(uint32_t (&R)[32]);
#ifndef IH_R16_SPLIT
#define IH_R16_SPLIT 0
#endif
#if IH_R16_SPLIT
// THE SPLIT FORM (GPU C, 15 Oct 2026, main's Turing order): the search kernel runs the init and the loop alone and writes S and the
// octet's 256 registers to a state buffer (264 words an attempt: S, then lane l's 32 registers at 8 + 32 l); ih_r16_mix hashes the
// mix M and the final digest F one attempt a thread at full lane width (the mix's 17 compressions ran on lane 0 of each octet with
// seven lanes idle: 30 to 37 percent of the 2060's hash time by the nomix figure). The pointer is set by the host through the module global.
__device__ uint32_t* ih_r16_state;
#endif
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r16_tag(uint8_t* p, int which) {
    const char* t; uint32_t n;
    switch (which) { case 0: t = "IgneumHash/R0F/init"; n = 19; break; default: t = "IgneumHash/R0F/mix"; n = 18; break; }
    p[0] = (uint8_t)n;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[1 + i] = (uint8_t)t[i];
    return n + 1;
}
// one BLAKE3 chunk's chaining value (hash mode, chunk counter t, not the root)
__device__ void ih_b3_chunk_cv(const uint8_t* msg, uint32_t len, uint64_t t, uint32_t cv[8]) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
    const uint32_t nblocks = len == 0 ? 1u : (len + 63u) / 64u;
    for (uint32_t b = 0; b < nblocks; ++b) {
        uint32_t m[16];
        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) m[i] = 0;
        const uint32_t off = 64u * b;
        const uint32_t blen = (b + 1 == nblocks) ? (len - off) : 64u;
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < blen; ++i) m[i >> 2] |= (uint32_t)msg[off + i] << (8 * (i & 3));
        const uint32_t flags = (b == 0 ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u) | (b + 1 == nblocks ? B3_CHUNK_END : 0u);
        uint32_t o[16];
        b3_compress_ctr(cv, m, t, blen, flags, o);
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv[i] = o[i];
    }
}
extern "C" __global__ void IH_SEARCH_BOUNDS
ih_search(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint4* __restrict__ dataset, uint32_t items_unused,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ scratch, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ results, uint32_t max_results) {
    const uint32_t lane = threadIdx.x & 7u;
    const uint32_t mask = 0xFFu << (threadIdx.x & 24);
    const uint32_t slot = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) >> 3;
    const uint32_t slots = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) >> 3;
    (void)scratch; (void)items_unused;
    // P = BLAKE3(params) once per thread; the seed prefix tag || C || V || P || H (96 bytes), as every version
    uint8_t buf[IH_R16_SPLIT ? 128 : 1088];   // the split form hashes the mix elsewhere: the seed's 104 bytes alone
    uint32_t o[16];
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, envelope, IH_PARAMS_LEN, 0, o);
    uint8_t prefix[96];
    uint32_t n = ih_tag(prefix, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) prefix[n + i] = envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + i];
    n += 12;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)o[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { const uint32_t w = params[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)w; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    const uint32_t Kw[8] = {IH_R16_K0, IH_R16_K1, IH_R16_K2, IH_R16_K3, IH_R16_K4, IH_R16_K5, IH_R16_K6, IH_R16_K7};
    // the init message LP(init) || K || S: the tag and K fixed per module, S per nonce at byte 52
    uint8_t im[96];
    const uint32_t in0 = ih_r16_tag(im, 0);
#pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { im[in0 + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)Kw[k]; im[in0 + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(Kw[k] >> 8); im[in0 + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(Kw[k] >> 16); im[in0 + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(Kw[k] >> 24); }
    for (uint32_t gi = slot; gi < count; gi += slots) {
        const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
        uint32_t S[8], M[8], F[8];
        for (int i = 0; i < 96; ++i) buf[i] = prefix[i];
        put_u64be(buf + 96, nonce);
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, 104, 0, o);
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
        uint32_t R[32];
        {
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { im[in0 + 32 + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; im[in0 + 33 + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); im[in0 + 34 + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); im[in0 + 35 + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, im, in0 + 64, 2u * lane, o);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) R[k] = o[k];
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, im, in0 + 64, 2u * lane + 1u, o);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) R[16 + k] = o[k];
        }
#pragma unroll 1
        for (uint32_t it = 0; it < IH_ROUNDS / 16u; ++it) {
            uint4 L[32];
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t b = 0; b < 4u; ++b) {
#pragma unroll
                for (uint32_t q = 0; q < 4u; ++q) {
                    const uint32_t x = __shfl_sync(mask, R[8u * b], (4u * b + q) & 7u, 8);
                    const uint32_t addr = (x ^ Kw[(4u * b + q) & 7u]) & (IH_N_ITEMS - 1u);   // the reference's (16 i + 4 b + q) mod 8: 16 i vanishes mod 8
                    const uint4* p = dataset + (uint64_t)addr * (IH_ITEM_BYTES / 16u) + 2u * lane;
                    L[2u * (4u * b + q)] = ih_ldx(p);
                    L[2u * (4u * b + q) + 1u] = ih_ldx(p + 1);
                }
            }
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t b = 0; b < 4u; ++b) {
#pragma unroll
                for (uint32_t q = 0; q < 4u; ++q) {
                    const uint4 w0 = L[2u * (4u * b + q)], w1 = L[2u * (4u * b + q) + 1u];
                    R[8u * b + 0u] ^= w0.x ^ Kw[(0u + q) & 7u]; R[8u * b + 1u] ^= w0.y ^ Kw[(1u + q) & 7u];
                    R[8u * b + 2u] ^= w0.z ^ Kw[(2u + q) & 7u]; R[8u * b + 3u] ^= w0.w ^ Kw[(3u + q) & 7u];
                    R[8u * b + 4u] ^= w1.x ^ Kw[(4u + q) & 7u]; R[8u * b + 5u] ^= w1.y ^ Kw[(5u + q) & 7u];
                    R[8u * b + 6u] ^= w1.z ^ Kw[(6u + q) & 7u]; R[8u * b + 7u] ^= w1.w ^ Kw[(7u + q) & 7u];
                }
            }
            ih_r16_prog(R);
        }
#if IH_R16_SPLIT
        {   // the split form: the octet's state to the buffer (lane l its 32 registers as eight 16-byte stores, the octet 1 KB contiguous), lane 0 the seed S
            uint32_t* stp = ih_r16_state + (size_t)gi * 264u; uint4* r4 = (uint4*)(stp + 8u + 32u * lane);
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) r4[k] = make_uint4(R[4 * k], R[4 * k + 1], R[4 * k + 2], R[4 * k + 3]);
            if (lane == 0) { ((uint4*)stp)[0] = make_uint4(S[0], S[1], S[2], S[3]); ((uint4*)stp)[1] = make_uint4(S[4], S[5], S[6], S[7]); }
            (void)M; (void)F;
        }
#else
        // M = BLAKE3(LP(mix) || K || R[0][0..32) .. R[7][0..32) LE): 19 + 32 + 1,024 bytes, gathered by lane 0
        uint32_t mn = 0;
        if (lane == 0) {
            mn = ih_r16_tag(buf, 1);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[mn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)Kw[k]; buf[mn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(Kw[k] >> 8); buf[mn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(Kw[k] >> 16); buf[mn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(Kw[k] >> 24); }
            mn += 32;
        }
#ifndef IH_R16_NOMIX
#define IH_R16_NOMIX 0
#endif
#if IH_R16_NOMIX
        // nomix=1 (GPU C, 15 Oct 2026): a FIGURE experiment, inexact: the mix's 1,075-byte BLAKE3 on lane 0 skipped (M the lane's first registers), the mix's cost by difference
        if (lane == 0) { for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = R[k] ^ Kw[k]; }
#else
        for (int l = 0; l < 8; ++l) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) {
                const uint32_t w = __shfl_sync(mask, R[k], l, 8);
                if (lane == 0) { const uint32_t pos = mn + 128 * l + 4 * k; buf[pos] = (uint8_t)w; buf[pos + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); buf[pos + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); buf[pos + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
            }
        }
#endif
        if (lane == 0) {
#if !IH_R16_NOMIX
            const uint32_t total = mn + 1024u;   // 1,075 bytes: chunk 0 the first 1,024, chunk 1 the rest, the root over the two
            uint32_t cv0[8], cv1[8], pm[16];
            ih_b3_chunk_cv(buf, 1024u, 0, cv0);
            ih_b3_chunk_cv(buf + 1024u, total - 1024u, 1, cv1);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { pm[i] = cv0[i]; pm[8 + i] = cv1[i]; }
            b3_compress_ctr(c_b3_iv, pm, 0, 64u, B3_PARENT | B3_ROOT, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
#endif
            // F = BLAKE3(tag(T_FINAL) || C || V || P || S || M)
            uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
            for (int i = 0; i < 44; ++i) buf[fn + i] = prefix[20 + i];
            fn += 44;
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
            fn += 32;
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
            fn += 32;
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
            bool le = true;
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
                const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
                if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
                if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
            }
            if (le) {
                const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
                if (rs < max_results) {
                    uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                    e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
                }
            }
        }
#endif   // IH_R16_SPLIT
        __syncwarp(mask);
    }
}
#if IH_R16_SPLIT
// the mix message as words (no byte buffer): tag(19 bytes) || K || R (Y = K || R, 264 words): word 0..3 the tag's bytes, word 4 the tag's
// last three bytes and Y[0]'s low byte, word w (5 on) = (Y[w-5] >> 8) | (Y[w-4] << 24); 1,075 bytes = chunk 0 (words 0..255) and chunk 1
// (words 256..268, 51 bytes), the root over the two chaining values
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r16_yw(const uint32_t* __restrict__ stp, const uint32_t* Kw, uint32_t i) { return i < 8u ? Kw[i] : (i < 264u ? stp[i] : 0u); }
__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ih_r16_mw(const uint32_t* __restrict__ stp, const uint32_t* Kw, const uint8_t* tg, uint32_t w) {
    if (w < 4u) return (uint32_t)tg[4 * w] | ((uint32_t)tg[4 * w + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)tg[4 * w + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)tg[4 * w + 3] << 24);
    if (w == 4u) return (uint32_t)tg[16] | ((uint32_t)tg[17] << 8) | ((uint32_t)tg[18] << 16) | ((ih_r16_yw(stp, Kw, 0) & 255u) << 24);
    return (ih_r16_yw(stp, Kw, w - 5u) >> 8) | (ih_r16_yw(stp, Kw, w - 4u) << 24);
}
extern "C" __global__ void __launch_bounds__(128)
ih_r16_mix(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope, uint32_t* __restrict__ results, uint32_t max_results) {
    const uint32_t gi = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (gi >= count) return;
    const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + gi;
    const uint32_t* stp = ih_r16_state + (size_t)gi * 264u;
    const uint32_t Kw[8] = {IH_R16_K0, IH_R16_K1, IH_R16_K2, IH_R16_K3, IH_R16_K4, IH_R16_K5, IH_R16_K6, IH_R16_K7};
    uint8_t tg[20]; ih_r16_tag(tg, 1);
    uint32_t o[16], M[8], F[8], S[8];
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = stp[k];
    {
        uint32_t cv0[8], cv1[8], m[16], pm[16];
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv0[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
        for (uint32_t b = 0; b < 16u; ++b) {
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 16u; ++i) m[i] = ih_r16_mw(stp, Kw, tg, 16u * b + i);
            const uint32_t flags = (b == 0 ? B3_CHUNK_START : 0u) | (b == 15u ? B3_CHUNK_END : 0u);
            b3_compress_ctr(cv0, m, 0, 64u, flags, o);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv0[i] = o[i];
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv1[i] = c_b3_iv[i];
#pragma unroll
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 16u; ++i) m[i] = i < 13u ? ih_r16_mw(stp, Kw, tg, 256u + i) : 0u;   // 51 bytes: words 256..268, the last three bytes
        b3_compress_ctr(cv1, m, 1, 51u, B3_CHUNK_START | B3_CHUNK_END, o);
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) cv1[i] = o[i];
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { pm[i] = cv0[i]; pm[8 + i] = cv1[i]; }
        b3_compress_ctr(c_b3_iv, pm, 0, 64u, B3_PARENT | B3_ROOT, o);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
    }
    // F = BLAKE3(tag(T_FINAL) || C || V || P || S || M): the prefix's bytes 20..63 are C || V || P (P = BLAKE3(params))
    uint8_t buf[160];
    uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) buf[fn + i] = envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + i];
    fn += 12;
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, envelope, IH_PARAMS_LEN, 0, o);
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)o[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 24); }
    fn += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
    fn += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
    fn += 32;
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
    bool le = true;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
        const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
        if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
        if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
    }
    if (le) {
        const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
        if (rs < max_results) {
            uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
            e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
        }
    }
}
#endif   // IH_R16_SPLIT
#else
extern "C" __global__ void IH_SEARCH_BOUNDS
ih_search(uint64_t start_nonce, uint32_t count, const uint4* __restrict__ dataset, uint32_t items_unused,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ scratch, const uint32_t* __restrict__ params, const uint8_t* __restrict__ envelope,
          uint32_t* __restrict__ results, uint32_t max_results
#if IH_R0E_ST
          , const uint32_t* __restrict__ r0e_st
#endif
#if IH_TRACE
          , uint32_t* __restrict__ ih_trace   // the trace mode (--tune trace=1): nonce 0's lane state after init, then per round the index, the state after the octet programme and at the round's end
#endif
#if IH_SPLIT
          , uint32_t* __restrict__ state, uint32_t* __restrict__ shares, uint32_t phase
#endif
          ) {
#if IH_R0E
    // version 10 (SPEC-R0E-v0.md, the octet programme): the fetched item staged per octet for the item-sourced operand and the
    // octet's 16 x 8 tile for the transpose; the per-nonce 16 KB gather table rides on the scratch buffer (slot x IH_R0E_TABLE_WORDS)
    __shared__ uint32_t ih_r0e_item[(IH_BLOCK / 8u) * IH_W4];
    __shared__ uint32_t ih_r0e_tile[(IH_BLOCK / 8u) * 128u];
#if IH_R0E_SHARED
    __shared__ uint32_t ih_r0e_tab[(IH_BLOCK / 8u) * IH_R0E_TABLE_WORDS];   // main's table-size cells: the per-nonce table in shared memory
#define IH_R0E_TABLE (ih_r0e_tab + (threadIdx.x >> IH_OCT_SHIFT) * IH_R0E_TABLE_WORDS)
#else
#define IH_R0E_TABLE (scratch + (uint64_t)slot * IH_R0E_TABLE_WORDS)
#endif
#endif
#if IH_LANES16
    const uint32_t lane = threadIdx.x & 15u;
    const uint32_t mask = 0xFFFFu << (threadIdx.x & 16);
    const uint32_t slot = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) >> 4;
    const uint32_t slots = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) >> 4;
#elif IH_OCTET32
    const uint32_t lane = (threadIdx.x >> 2) & 7u;        // the octet lane this quad replicates
    const uint32_t mask = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    const uint32_t slot = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) >> 5;
    const uint32_t slots = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) >> 5;
#else
    const uint32_t lane = threadIdx.x & 7u;
    const uint32_t mask = 0xFFu << (threadIdx.x & 24);
    const uint32_t slot = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) >> 3;
    const uint32_t slots = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) >> 3;
#endif
    uint32_t* sl = scratch + (uint64_t)slot * (IH_SLICE_WORDS * 8u) + lane;  // word j of this lane at sl[8j]

    // P = BLAKE3(params) once per thread (IH_PARAMS_LEN bytes); the seed prefix tag || C || V || P || H (96 bytes)
    uint8_t buf[704];
    uint32_t o[16];
    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, envelope, IH_PARAMS_LEN, 0, o);
    uint8_t prefix[96];
    uint32_t n = ih_tag(prefix, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) prefix[n + i] = envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + i];
#if IH_NOFETCH
    const uint32_t e8w = (uint32_t)envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + 12 + 4 * (lane & 7u)] | ((uint32_t)envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + 13 + 4 * (lane & 7u)] << 8) |
                         ((uint32_t)envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + 14 + 4 * (lane & 7u)] << 16) | ((uint32_t)envelope[IH_PARAMS_LEN + 15 + 4 * (lane & 7u)] << 24);
#endif
    n += 12;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)o[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(o[k] >> 24); }
    n += 32;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { const uint32_t w = params[k]; prefix[n + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)w; prefix[n + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); prefix[n + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); prefix[n + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
    n += 32;  // n = 96
#if IH_TUNE_WORDS
#if IH_TUNE_COLD
    // The ladder's family 2 (cold state to shared memory, 10 Oct 2026): the 41 words the T7 tail holds as constants in every
    // thread (the seed prefix's 24, the lane, mix and final tags' 6 + 5 + 6) are identical across the kernel, so they are
    // staged once per block in shared memory and read by broadcast (one address per warp, no bank conflict); 41 registers
    // of live state leave every thread for the whole nonce loop. The values are the register form's, word for word.
    __shared__ uint32_t ih_cold[41];
    {
        const uint32_t tid = threadIdx.x;
        if (tid < 24) ih_cold[tid] = (uint32_t)prefix[4 * tid] | ((uint32_t)prefix[4 * tid + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)prefix[4 * tid + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)prefix[4 * tid + 3] << 24);
        else if (tid < 30) ih_cold[tid] = ih_tag_word(4, tid - 24);
        else if (tid < 35) ih_cold[tid] = ih_tag_word(5, tid - 30);
        else if (tid < 41) ih_cold[tid] = ih_tag_word(6, tid - 35);
        __syncthreads();
    }
    const uint32_t* const pw = ih_cold;        // pw[0 .. 24)
    const uint32_t* const tl = ih_cold + 24;   // tl[0 .. 6)
    const uint32_t* const tm = ih_cold + 30;   // tm[0 .. 5)
    const uint32_t* const tf = ih_cold + 35;   // tf[0 .. 6)
#else
    // T7: the seed prefix as 24 little-endian words, the tag words of the lane, mix and final messages, once per thread
    uint32_t pw[24];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 24; ++i) pw[i] = (uint32_t)prefix[4 * i] | ((uint32_t)prefix[4 * i + 1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)prefix[4 * i + 2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)prefix[4 * i + 3] << 24);
    uint32_t tl[6], tm[5], tf[6];
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; ++i) tl[i] = ih_tag_word(4, i);
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) tm[i] = ih_tag_word(5, i);
#pragma unroll
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; ++i) tf[i] = ih_tag_word(6, i);
#endif
#endif

#if IH_TUNE_TAIL
    __shared__ uint32_t ih_tail[(IH_BLOCK / IH_HW) * IH_TUNE_TAIL * IH_TS];   // one slot set per lane group of the block
    uint32_t* const tb = ih_tail + (threadIdx.x / IH_HW) * (IH_TUNE_TAIL * IH_TS);   // this group's slots
    uint32_t nb = 0;       // staged nonces in the current batch
    uint32_t gid0 = 0;     // the batch's first gid (slot i holds nonce start + gid0 + i slots)
#endif
#ifndef IH_QUEUE
#define IH_QUEUE 0
#endif
    // family 3 (IH_QUEUE 1): a persistent kernel with a device-side nonce queue; lane 0 of each group takes the next nonce
    // from the counter behind the results (results[1 + max_results * RESULT_WORDS]) and broadcasts it, so a group that
    // finishes early takes the next nonce at once and no group idles on the grid stride; the counter is cleared with the
    // results before the launch. IH_QUEUE 0 is the grid-stride loop.
#if IH_QUEUE
    uint32_t* qctr = results + 1 + max_results * RESULT_WORDS;
    for (;;) {
        uint32_t gid = 0;
        if (lane == 0) gid = atomicAdd(qctr, 1u);
        gid = __shfl_sync(mask, gid, IH_SRC8(0), IH_W8);
        if (gid >= count) break;
#elif IH_R0E_V14
    // version 14's tile is a warp-collective mma.sync: the four octets of a warp must stay in the loop together, so a lane group past
    // the count keeps computing on a wrapped nonce (its results discarded by ih_live) until the whole warp is done (the 13 of 13
    // mismatches of 01:1x UK 13 Oct: the vectors verb's few nonces left most lanes exited and the mma ran with an incomplete warp)
    for (uint32_t gid = slot; __any_sync(0xFFFFFFFFu, gid < count); gid += slots) {
        const bool ih_live = gid < count;
#else
    for (uint32_t gid = slot; gid < count; gid += slots) {
        const bool ih_live = true;
#endif
#if IH_QUEUE
        const bool ih_live = true;
#endif
        const uint64_t nonce = start_nonce + (uint64_t)(ih_live ? gid : gid % (count ? count : 1u));
        uint32_t S[8];
        uint32_t R[16];
#if IH_R0E_ST
        uint32_t ih_nonce_off = 0;
#endif
#if IH_CHAINS == 2
        const bool chainb = gid + slots < count;   // chain B, the batch's second nonce, active only inside the range
        uint32_t S2[8], R2[16];
#endif
#if IH_TUNE_TAIL
        {
            // S for the batch's N nonces (this one and the next N - 1 of this group), one per lane, at the batch's first nonce
            if (nb == 0) {
                gid0 = gid;
                if (lane < IH_TUNE_TAIL && gid + lane * slots < count) {
                    const uint64_t nl = start_nonce + (uint64_t)(gid + lane * slots);
                    const uint32_t nhi = bswap32((uint32_t)(nl >> 32)), nlo = bswap32((uint32_t)nl);
                    b3_words_xof<104>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 24 ? pw[i] : (i == 24 ? nhi : nlo); }, o);
                    uint32_t* sw = tb + lane * IH_TS;
#pragma unroll
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) sw[k] = o[k];
                }
                __syncwarp(mask);
            }
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = tb[nb * IH_TS + k];
            // L = BLAKE3xof(tag(T_LANES) || S, 512): this lane takes block l (as the words path)
            b3_words_xof<53>(lane / (IH_HW / 8u), [&](uint32_t i) {
                if (i < 5) return tl[i];
                const uint32_t j = i - 5;
                if (j == 0) return tl[5] | (S[0] << 8);
                if (j < 8) return (S[j - 1] >> 24) | (S[j] << 8);
                return S[7] >> 24;
            }, o);
#if IH_LANES16
            // a select, never a lane-indexed read of o[] (that puts o in local memory: STACK 96, 18 STL in the first build)
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) R[k] = (lane & 1u) ? o[8 + k] : o[k];
#else
            for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) R[k] = o[k];
#endif
#if IH_CHAINS == 2
            // chain B's state from slot 1
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S2[k] = chainb ? tb[1 * IH_TS + k] : S[k];
            b3_words_xof<53>(lane / (IH_HW / 8u), [&](uint32_t i) {
                if (i < 5) return tl[i];
                const uint32_t j = i - 5;
                if (j == 0) return tl[5] | (S2[0] << 8);
                if (j < 8) return (S2[j - 1] >> 24) | (S2[j] << 8);
                return S2[7] >> 24;
            }, o);
#if IH_LANES16
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) R2[k] = (lane & 1u) ? o[8 + k] : o[k];
#else
            for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) R2[k] = o[k];
#endif
#endif
        }
#elif IH_TUNE_WORDS == 1 || IH_TUNE_WORDS == 2  // the seed and lane initialisation from words (words=2 bisects: this block alone)
        {
            // S = BLAKE3(prefix || u64be(N)): 26 words, the nonce big-endian in words 24 and 25
            const uint32_t nhi = bswap32((uint32_t)(nonce >> 32)), nlo = bswap32((uint32_t)nonce);
            b3_words_xof<104>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return i < 24 ? pw[i] : (i == 24 ? nhi : nlo); }, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
            // L = BLAKE3xof(tag(T_LANES) || S, 512): the 21-byte tag then S at byte 21 (word 5, byte 1); this lane takes block l
            b3_words_xof<53>(lane, [&](uint32_t i) {
                if (i < 5) return tl[i];
                const uint32_t j = i - 5;  // j = 0: tag byte 20 | S0 << 8; j = 1..7: S[j-1] >> 24 | S[j] << 8; j = 8: S7 >> 24
                if (j == 0) return tl[5] | (S[0] << 8);
                if (j < 8) return (S[j - 1] >> 24) | (S[j] << 8);
                return S[7] >> 24;
            }, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) R[k] = o[k];
        }
#else
        // S = BLAKE3(prefix || u64be(N))
        for (int i = 0; i < 96; ++i) buf[i] = prefix[i];
        put_u64be(buf + 96, nonce);
        b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, 104, 0, o);
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = o[k];
        // 4.1 lane init: L = BLAKE3xof(tag(T_LANES) || S, 512); this lane takes output block l
        {
            uint8_t lm[64];
            uint32_t ln = ih_tag(lm, 4);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { lm[ln + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; lm[ln + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); lm[ln + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); lm[ln + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
            b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, lm, ln + 32, lane, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) R[k] = o[k];
        }
#endif
        uint32_t P8[8] = {0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};   // draw revision 2: no program seed (the item is the programme); the argument stays for the signature
        // 4.2 scratch fill
#if IH_PAD
#pragma unroll 1
        for (uint32_t j = 0; j < IH_SLICE_WORDS; ++j) {
            const uint32_t jm = j & 15u;
            uint32_t a = R[jm];
            const uint32_t b = R[(j + 5) & 15u];
            a = (a ^ ih_rotl(b, 13)) * IH_MUL;
            a = a + (b >> 7);
            R[jm] = a;
            sl[8 * j] = a ^ R[(j + 11) & 15u];
        }
#endif
#if IH_GRIND
        const long long grind_t0 = clock64();
#endif
        // 4.3 first address
#if IH_LANES16
        // relation lane 0 register 0 is hardware lane 0 register 0; relation lane 4 register 8 is hardware lane 9 register 0
        uint32_t idx = IH_ADDR(__shfl_sync(mask, R[0], 0, 16) ^ __shfl_sync(mask, R[0], 9, 16));
        ih_prefetch_l2((const uint4*)((const uint32_t*)dataset + (uint64_t)idx * IH_W4) + (lane >> 1));
#else
        uint32_t idx = IH_ADDR(__shfl_sync(mask, R[0], IH_SRC8(0), IH_W8) ^ __shfl_sync(mask, R[8], IH_SRC8(4), IH_W8));
#endif
#if IH_SPLIT
        // Split execution (GUARD-C-CLOSURE.md 1.4, the renter's side; split=1 with bit 10): the walk runs in IH_KEYED_S + 1
        // phases, one launch each over the same grid. Phase 0 computes S and the lane state, stores them in this nonce's
        // state slot and writes the round-0 request m_0 = S; phase p (1 .. S) loads the slot, runs rounds [kr(p - 1), kr(p))
        // with the oracle's share of slot p - 1 entering at round kr(p - 1), then stores the state and writes m_p = R[l][0]
        // (phase S runs to ROUNDS and finishes the hash). kr(i) = ceil(i ROUNDS / S), the round of keyed index i. The slot
        // (144 words): S at 0 .. 7 (lane l writes word l), lane l's R at 8 + 16 l, idx at 136 (lane 0).
        uint32_t* const st = state + (uint64_t)gid * 144u;
        const uint32_t r_from = phase == 0 ? 0u : (uint32_t)(((uint64_t)(phase - 1) * IH_ROUNDS + IH_KEYED_S - 1) / IH_KEYED_S);
        const uint32_t r_to = phase >= IH_KEYED_S ? IH_ROUNDS : (uint32_t)(((uint64_t)phase * IH_ROUNDS + IH_KEYED_S - 1) / IH_KEYED_S);
        if (phase == 0) {
            st[lane] = S[lane];
#pragma unroll
            for (int q = 0; q < 16; ++q) st[8 + 16 * lane + q] = R[q];
            if (lane == 0) st[136] = idx;
            shares[((uint64_t)gid * IH_KEYED_S) * 8u + lane] = S[lane];   // the round-0 request, m_0 = S
            continue;
        }
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) S[k] = st[k];
#pragma unroll
        for (int q = 0; q < 16; ++q) R[q] = st[8 + 16 * lane + q];
        idx = st[136];
#else
        const uint32_t r_from = 0u, r_to = IH_ROUNDS;
#endif
#if IH_LANES16
#elif IH_VERSION == 4 || IH_VERSION == 6
        ih_prefetch_l2((const uint4*)((const uint32_t*)dataset + (uint64_t)idx * IH_W4) + (lane * IH_W4 / 32u));
#else
        ih_prefetch_l2(dataset + (uint64_t)idx * IH_ITEM_U4 + IH_SUB * lane);
#endif
#if IH_LANES16 && IH_CHAINS == 2
        uint32_t idx2 = IH_ADDR(__shfl_sync(mask, R2[0], 0, 16) ^ __shfl_sync(mask, R2[0], 9, 16));
#pragma unroll 1
        for (uint32_t r = 0; r < IH_ROUNDS; ++r) {
            uint2 w2, w2b;
            ih_v4_load16(w2, dataset, idx, lane);
            ih_v4_load16(w2b, dataset, idx2, lane);
            ih_v4_compute16(R, w2, idx, lane, mask, r);
            ih_v4_compute16(R2, w2b, idx2, lane, mask, r);
        }
#elif IH_LANES16
#pragma unroll 1
        for (uint32_t r = 0; r < IH_ROUNDS; ++r) {
            uint2 w2;
            ih_v4_load16(w2, dataset, idx, lane);
            ih_v4_compute16(R, w2, idx, lane, mask, r);
        }
#elif IH_CHAINS == 2
        uint32_t idx2 = IH_ADDR(__shfl_sync(mask, R2[0], IH_SRC8(0), IH_W8) ^ __shfl_sync(mask, R2[8], IH_SRC8(4), IH_W8));
#pragma unroll 1
        for (uint32_t r = 0; r < IH_ROUNDS; ++r) {
            uint32_t wl[IH_W4 / 8u], wl2[IH_W4 / 8u];
#if IH_NOFETCH
            ih_v4_nofetch(wl, R, lane, e8w); ih_v4_nofetch(wl2, R2, lane, e8w);
#else
            ih_v4_load(wl, dataset, idx, lane);
            ih_v4_load(wl2, dataset, idx2, lane);
#endif
            ih_v4_compute(R, wl, idx, lane, mask, r, P8);
            ih_v4_compute(R2, wl2, idx2, lane, mask, r, P8);
        }
#else
#if IH_TRACE
        if (gid == 0u && IH_OCT_ONE) { for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) ih_trace[lane * 16u + (uint32_t)k] = R[k]; }
#endif
#pragma unroll 1
        for (uint32_t r = r_from; r < r_to; ++r) {   // the whole walk, or one phase of the split (r_from .. r_to)
#if IH_TRACE
            if (gid == 0u && IH_OCT_ONE && lane == 0u) { ih_trace[128u + r * 258u] = idx; ih_trace[129u + r * 258u] = 0u; }
            if (blockIdx.x == 0u && threadIdx.x == 0u) ih_tr_round = r;   // the per-op dump takes round 0 of the first nonce
            __syncwarp(mask);
#endif
#if IH_VERSION == 4 || IH_VERSION == 6
            // Version 4 (Strata, SPEC-R0A-v0.md 0.4): the whole item w[0 .. n), n = W / 4, lane l holding w[8 k + l] in wl[k]
            // (eight lanes read 32 contiguous bytes per instruction). 1. absorb: R[j mod 8][(j div 8) mod 16] += w[j], so lane l
            // adds wl[k] to R[k mod 16]. 2. the item word h by the balanced domain-separated tree over the leaves
            // z[j] = w[j] ^ ((j + 1) MUL), node(x, y) = ((x ^ rotl(y, 13)) MUL) + (y >> 7), pairs (2i, 2i + 1) in order; the live
            // nodes of a level with m entries sit at lane (i mod 8), register (i div 8). 3. the 4.4 cell once per lane with w = h
            // for every quad, then the permutation. 4. the exchange. 5. idx = (R[g][0] ^ h) mod N_ITEMS, g = (r + 1) mod 8.
            // IH_LOADS: the physical load form (convergence sweep 5; Riptide RX-D): 32 = IH_W4/8 LDG.32 per lane, each
            // instruction 32 contiguous bytes (word j = l + 8 k lands in lane l's wl[k] directly); 64 = LDG.64, 128 = LDG.128
            // per lane per 128-byte sub-item (coalesced 64 or 128 bytes per instruction) with the words moved to the absorb
            // layout by shuffles; 256 = the Turing overfetch form at W = 128 (the aligned 256-byte pair of items loaded as two
            // LDG.128 per lane, the other item discarded). Every form lands the same words in the same registers.
            const uint32_t* item32 = (const uint32_t*)dataset + (uint64_t)idx * IH_W4;
            uint32_t wl[IH_W4 / 8u];
#if IH_LOADS == 32
#pragma unroll
#if IH_NOFETCH
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < IH_W4 / 8u; ++k) wl[k] = ih_rotl(R[k & 15u], 7u) ^ ((8u * k + lane + 1u) * IH_MUL) ^ e8w;   // bit 9: the words from the lane state and the epoch seed's word for this lane (a164a662)
            (void)item32;
#else
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < IH_W4 / 8u; ++k) wl[k] = ih_ldx(item32 + 8u * k + lane);
#endif
#elif IH_LOADS == 64
            // sub-item s, instruction t: lane l holds words 32 s + 16 t + 2 l, + 1; word l + 8 k sits in s = k / 4, t = (k % 4) / 2,
            // source lane 4 ((k % 4) % 2) + l / 2, component l % 2
            uint2 u2[IH_W4 / 32u][2];
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t s = 0; s < IH_W4 / 32u; ++s)
#pragma unroll
                for (uint32_t t = 0; t < 2; ++t) u2[s][t] = ih_ldx((const uint2*)(item32 + 32u * s) + 8u * t + lane);
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < IH_W4 / 8u; ++k) {
                const uint32_t src = 4u * ((k % 4u) % 2u) + (lane >> 1);
                const uint32_t x = __shfl_sync(mask, u2[k / 4u][(k % 4u) / 2u].x, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8);
                const uint32_t y = __shfl_sync(mask, u2[k / 4u][(k % 4u) / 2u].y, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8);
                wl[k] = (lane & 1u) ? y : x;
            }
#elif IH_LOADS == 128 || IH_LOADS == 256
            // sub-item s: lane l holds words 32 s + 4 l .. + 3; word l + 8 k sits in s = k / 4, source lane 2 (k % 4) + l / 4,
            // component l % 4
            uint4 u4[IH_W4 / 32u];
#if IH_LOADS == 256
            {
                const uint4* pair = (const uint4*)((const uint32_t*)dataset + (uint64_t)(idx & ~1u) * IH_W4);
                const uint4 p0 = ih_ldx(pair + lane), p1 = ih_ldx(pair + 8u + lane);
                u4[0] = (idx & 1u) ? p1 : p0;
            }
#else
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t s = 0; s < IH_W4 / 32u; ++s) u4[s] = ih_ldx((const uint4*)(item32 + 32u * s) + lane);
#endif
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < IH_W4 / 8u; ++k) {
                const uint32_t src = 2u * (k % 4u) + (lane >> 2);
                const uint4 v = u4[k / 4u];
                const uint32_t x = __shfl_sync(mask, v.x, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8), y = __shfl_sync(mask, v.y, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8);
                const uint32_t z = __shfl_sync(mask, v.z, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8), w = __shfl_sync(mask, v.w, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8);
                const uint32_t c = lane & 3u;
                wl[k] = c == 0 ? x : (c == 1 ? y : (c == 2 ? z : w));
            }
#elif IH_LOADS == 512
            // the fused form (Riptide RX-C): lane l reads its own W/8 contiguous bytes as T = W/128 LDG.128 at a W/8-byte stride
            // across lanes (every instruction touches every 128-byte line of the item); word l + 8 k sits in source lane
            // 2 k / T, uint4 t = l / 4 + 2 (k mod (T / 2)), component l % 4: eight shuffles per k
            uint4 uf[IH_W4 / 32u];
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t t = 0; t < IH_W4 / 32u; ++t) uf[t] = ih_ldx((const uint4*)item32 + (IH_W4 / 32u) * lane + t);
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < IH_W4 / 8u; ++k) {
                const uint32_t T = IH_W4 / 32u;
                const uint32_t src = (2u * k) / T;
                const uint32_t t0 = 2u * (k % (T / 2u > 0 ? T / 2u : 1u));
                uint4 a = uf[0], bq = uf[0];
#pragma unroll
                for (uint32_t t = 0; t < IH_W4 / 32u; ++t) { a = (t == t0) ? uf[t] : a; bq = (t == t0 + 1u) ? uf[t] : bq; }
                const uint32_t ax = __shfl_sync(mask, a.x, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8), ay = __shfl_sync(mask, a.y, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8), az = __shfl_sync(mask, a.z, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8), aw = __shfl_sync(mask, a.w, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8);
                const uint32_t bx = __shfl_sync(mask, bq.x, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8), by = __shfl_sync(mask, bq.y, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8), bz = __shfl_sync(mask, bq.z, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8), bw = __shfl_sync(mask, bq.w, IH_SRC8(src), IH_W8);
                const uint32_t c = lane & 3u;
                const uint32_t va = c == 0 ? ax : (c == 1 ? ay : (c == 2 ? az : aw));
                const uint32_t vb = c == 0 ? bx : (c == 1 ? by : (c == 2 ? bz : bw));
                wl[k] = (lane >> 2) ? vb : va;
            }
#else
#error "IH_LOADS must be 32, 64, 128, 256 or 512"
#endif
#if IH_BLEND && (IH_V8_F & 1024)
            // Version 8 bit 10 (SPEC-R0A-v0.md 0.9, the bond-keyed rounds): round r is keyed iff (r S) mod ROUNDS < S, its
            // index i = (r S) div ROUNDS. The share s_i = BLAKE3_keyed(sk, "bond" || LE32(i) || m_i), one 40-byte block
            // (CHUNK_START | CHUNK_END | ROOT | KEYED_HASH, counter 0); m_0 = S (the work seed), m_i for i >= 1 the eight lane
            // words R[l][0] at the round's entry (before the absorb). The share enters the item's words 0 .. 7 before the
            // absorb, the programme and the tree: word j = lane j's wl[0] (word l + 8 k sits in lane l's wl[k]). Every lane
            // of the group computes the compression (the warp issues it once either way) and takes its own word by a select,
            // never a lane-indexed read. The key is the 32-byte sk at params[16 .. 24) (the test secret for the vectors and
            // the bench; a real bond's secret through the same words). The branch is uniform across the warp.
            if ((r * IH_KEYED_S) % IH_ROUNDS < IH_KEYED_S) {
                const uint32_t ki = (r * IH_KEYED_S) / IH_ROUNDS;
#if IH_SPLIT
                // split execution (GUARD-C-CLOSURE.md 1.4, the renter): the share came back from the oracle into this
                // nonce's slot ki (lane word l = s_i[4 l ..]); the secret is not on this card
                wl[0] ^= shares[((uint64_t)gid * IH_KEYED_S + ki) * 8u + lane];
            }
#else
                uint32_t km[16], kk[8], ko[16];
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) kk[k] = params[16 + k];
                km[0] = 0x646E6F62u;   // "bond"
                km[1] = ki;
                if (ki == 0) {
#pragma unroll
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) km[2 + k] = S[k];
                } else {
#pragma unroll
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) km[2 + k] = __shfl_sync(mask, R[0], IH_SRC8(k), IH_W8);
                }
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 10; k < 16; ++k) km[k] = 0u;
                b3_compress_ctr(kk, km, 0, 40, B3_KEYED_HASH | B3_CHUNK_START | B3_CHUNK_END | B3_ROOT, ko);
                uint32_t sw = ko[0];
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 1; k < 8; ++k) sw = (lane == (uint32_t)k) ? ko[k] : sw;
                wl[0] ^= sw;
            }
#endif  // IH_SPLIT
#endif
            // version 6 (SPEC-R0A-v0.md 0.6): the leaves keyed by the register the word enters, as it stands before the absorb
            uint32_t z[IH_W4 / 8u];
#if IH_R0E && IH_R0E_V11
            if (r == 0) ih_r0e_fill(R, S, dataset, lane, mask, IH_R0E_TABLE);   // version 11: the gather table from the state before the first absorb
#if IH_R0E_ST
            if (r == 0) { ih_nonce_off = 0; for (uint32_t w = 0; w < 8u; ++w) { const uint32_t v = S[w]; ih_nonce_off = ih_rotl(ih_nonce_off ^ v, 5u) + v; } }   // the shared form's nonce-dependent row offset
#endif
#endif
#if IH_VERSION == 6 || IH_R0E_V11
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < IH_W4 / 8u; ++k) z[k] = wl[k] ^ R[k & 15u] ^ ((8u * k + lane + 1u) * IH_MUL);
#endif
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < IH_W4 / 8u; ++k) R[k & 15u] += wl[k];
#if IH_BLEND
            ih_v8_block(R, wl, lane, mask, IH_PAD ? sl : (uint32_t*)nullptr);   // step 1b (version 8, draw revision 2: the item is the programme)
#if IH_V8_F & 8192
#if IH_R0E
            // version 10 (SPEC-R0E-v0.md): the octet programme compiled in by the engine; the table built at the nonce's first round
#if !IH_R0E_V11
            if (r == 0) ih_r0e_table(S, lane, mask, IH_R0E_TABLE);   // version 10: the XOF table
#endif
#if IH_R0E_ST
            ih_r0e_octet(R, wl, lane, mask, ih_r0e_item + (threadIdx.x >> IH_OCT_SHIFT) * IH_W4, ih_r0e_tile + (threadIdx.x >> IH_OCT_SHIFT) * 128u, IH_R0E_TABLE, r0e_st, ih_nonce_off);
#else
            ih_r0e_octet(R, wl, lane, mask, ih_r0e_item + (threadIdx.x >> IH_OCT_SHIFT) * IH_W4, ih_r0e_tile + (threadIdx.x >> IH_OCT_SHIFT) * 128u, IH_R0E_TABLE);
#endif
#if IH_TRACE
            if (gid == 0u && IH_OCT_ONE) { for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) ih_trace[130u + r * 258u + lane * 16u + (uint32_t)k] = R[k]; }
#endif
#else
            ih_r0d_lane(R);   // version 9 (SPEC-R0D-v0.md): the period's programme on the lane's 16 registers, compiled in by the engine, after the walk-only constant and before the tree
#endif
#endif
#endif
#if IH_VERSION != 6 && !IH_R0E_V11
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < IH_W4 / 8u; ++k) z[k] = wl[k] ^ ((8u * k + lane + 1u) * IH_MUL);
#endif
            // levels: m = n, n/2, ..., 2; the destination (lane l, register q) takes z[2 i], z[2 i + 1] with i = 8 q + l,
            // from source lanes (2 l) and (2 l + 1) mod 8 at register 2 q + (l >= 4)
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t m = IH_W4; m >= 2u; m >>= 1) {
                const uint32_t nq = (m / 2u + 7u) / 8u;  // live destination registers
                uint32_t nz[IH_W4 / 8u];
#pragma unroll
                for (uint32_t q = 0; q < IH_W4 / 8u; ++q) {
                    if (q < nq) {
                        const uint32_t r0 = 2u * q, r1 = 2u * q + 1u;
                        const uint32_t s0 = (2u * lane) & 7u, s1 = (2u * lane + 1u) & 7u;
                        const uint32_t x0 = __shfl_sync(mask, r0 < IH_W4 / 8u ? z[r0 < IH_W4 / 8u ? r0 : 0] : 0u, IH_SRC8(s0), IH_W8);
                        const uint32_t x1 = __shfl_sync(mask, r1 < IH_W4 / 8u ? z[r1 < IH_W4 / 8u ? r1 : 0] : 0u, IH_SRC8(s0), IH_W8);
                        const uint32_t y0 = __shfl_sync(mask, r0 < IH_W4 / 8u ? z[r0 < IH_W4 / 8u ? r0 : 0] : 0u, IH_SRC8(s1), IH_W8);
                        const uint32_t y1 = __shfl_sync(mask, r1 < IH_W4 / 8u ? z[r1 < IH_W4 / 8u ? r1 : 0] : 0u, IH_SRC8(s1), IH_W8);
                        const uint32_t x = lane >= 4u ? x1 : x0, y = lane >= 4u ? y1 : y0;
                        nz[q] = ((x ^ ih_rotl(y, 13)) * IH_MUL) + (y >> 7);
                    } else nz[q] = 0u;
                }
#pragma unroll
                for (uint32_t q = 0; q < IH_W4 / 8u; ++q) z[q] = nz[q];
            }
#if IH_VERSION == 6 || IH_R0E_V11
            uint32_t h = __shfl_sync(mask, z[0], IH_SRC8(0), IH_W8);
            h ^= h >> 16; h *= IH_MUL; h ^= h >> 15;  // the root finaliser (0.6; version 11 the same)
#else
            const uint32_t h = __shfl_sync(mask, z[0], IH_SRC8(0), IH_W8);
#endif
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 4; ++k) {
                uint32_t a = R[4 * k], b = R[4 * k + 1], c = R[4 * k + 2], d = R[4 * k + 3];
                const uint32_t w = h;
#else
            // 4.4 item load and arithmetic: lane l's IH_SUB sub-blocks are uint4 IH_SUB * l + s of the item; the cell runs
            // once per sub-block in order (W = 128: one sub-block, the version 1 round)
            uint4 Dsub[IH_SUB];
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t s = 0; s < IH_SUB; ++s) Dsub[s] = ih_load_item(dataset + (uint64_t)idx * IH_ITEM_U4 + IH_SUB * lane + s);
#pragma unroll
            for (uint32_t s = 0; s < IH_SUB; ++s) {
            const uint4 D = Dsub[s];
            const uint32_t Dw[4] = {D.x, D.y, D.z, D.w};
#pragma unroll
            for (int k = 0; k < 4; ++k) {
                uint32_t a = R[4 * k], b = R[4 * k + 1], c = R[4 * k + 2], d = R[4 * k + 3];
                const uint32_t w = Dw[k];
#endif
                a = a + w;
                b = IH_OP1_ADD ? b + a : b ^ a;
                c = c + b * IH_MUL;
                d = ih_rotr(d ^ c, a & 31u);
                a = a ^ (d >> (w & 31u));
                b = ih_rotl(b + d, IH_R1);
                c = c ^ ih_rotl(a, IH_R2);
                d = IH_OP2_XOR ? d ^ (b * c) : d + b * c;
                R[4 * k] = a; R[4 * k + 1] = b; R[4 * k + 2] = c; R[4 * k + 3] = d;
            }
            {
                uint32_t T[16];
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) T[k] = R[k];
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) R[k] = T[(5 * k + 1) & 15];
            }
#if IH_VERSION != 4 && IH_VERSION != 6
            }  // sub-blocks
#endif
            // 4.5 scratch access
#if IH_PAD
            {
                const uint32_t line = R[0] & (IH_SLICE_LINES - 1u);
#pragma unroll
                for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
                    const uint32_t s = sl[8 * (4 * line + j)];
                    R[8 + j] = ih_rotl(R[8 + j] ^ s, 5) + R[j];
                    sl[8 * (4 * line + j)] = (s + R[12 + j]) ^ R[8 + j];
                }
            }
#endif
            // 4.5 exchange with snapshot semantics (every read is of the pre-exchange values)
            {
                const uint32_t p = lane ^ (1u << (r % 3u));
                const uint32_t q = (lane + 1u + (R[1] % 7u)) & 7u;
                const uint32_t xp0 = __shfl_sync(mask, R[0], IH_SRC8(p), IH_W8);
                const uint32_t xq1 = __shfl_sync(mask, R[1], IH_SRC8(q), IH_W8);
                const uint32_t xp2 = __shfl_sync(mask, R[2], IH_SRC8(p), IH_W8);
                const uint32_t xq3 = __shfl_sync(mask, R[3], IH_SRC8(q), IH_W8);
                R[0] = R[0] + xp0;
                R[1] = R[1] ^ xq1;
                R[2] = R[2] + ih_rotl(xp2, 11);
                R[3] = R[3] ^ ih_rotl(xq3, 23);
            }
            // 4.6 next address
#if IH_TRACE
            if (gid == 0u && IH_OCT_ONE) { for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) ih_trace[258u + r * 258u + lane * 16u + (uint32_t)k] = R[k]; }
#endif
#if IH_VERSION == 4 || IH_VERSION == 6
            idx = IH_ADDR(__shfl_sync(mask, R[0], IH_SRC8((r + 1) & 7u), IH_W8) ^ h);
            ih_prefetch_l2((const uint4*)((const uint32_t*)dataset + (uint64_t)idx * IH_W4) + (lane * IH_W4 / 32u));
#else
            idx = IH_ADDR(__shfl_sync(mask, R[0], IH_SRC8((r + 1) & 7u), IH_W8));
            ih_prefetch_l2(dataset + (uint64_t)idx * IH_ITEM_U4 + IH_SUB * lane);  // T2c: the next line toward L2 before the lanes ask
#endif
        }
#endif  // IH_CHAINS
#if IH_SPLIT
        if (phase < IH_KEYED_S) {
            // the phase ends at keyed round kr(phase): store the state and write the request m_phase = R[l][0] (lane l's word)
#pragma unroll
            for (int q = 0; q < 16; ++q) st[8 + 16 * lane + q] = R[q];
            if (lane == 0) st[136] = idx;
            shares[((uint64_t)gid * IH_KEYED_S + phase) * 8u + lane] = R[0];
            continue;
        }
#endif
#if IH_GRIND
        if (lane == 0) scratch[gid] = (uint32_t)(clock64() - grind_t0);
#endif
        // 4.7 scratch fold
        uint32_t f[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
#if IH_PAD
#pragma unroll 1
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < IH_SLICE_LINES; ++i) {
            const uint32_t s0 = sl[8 * (4 * i)], s1 = sl[8 * (4 * i + 1)], s2 = sl[8 * (4 * i + 2)], s3 = sl[8 * (4 * i + 3)];
            f[0] = ih_rotl(f[0] ^ s0, 9) + s1;
            f[1] = ih_rotl(f[1] ^ s1, 9) + s2;
            f[2] = ih_rotl(f[2] ^ s2, 9) + s3;
            f[3] = ih_rotl(f[3] ^ s3, 9) + s0;
        }
#endif
        // M = BLAKE3(tag(T_MIX) || S || ST), ST = R of every lane then (with a pad) f of every lane, little-endian: lane 0 gathers
        uint32_t M[8], F[8];
#if IH_TUNE_TAIL
        {
            (void)f; (void)nonce;
            // stage this nonce's state words: slot nb, word 8 + 16 l + q = lane l's R[q] (the order of ST)
            uint32_t* const sw = tb + nb * IH_TS;
#pragma unroll
#if IH_LANES16
            for (int q = 0; q < 8; ++q) sw[8 + 16 * (lane >> 1) + 8 * (lane & 1u) + q] = R[q];
#else
            for (int q = 0; q < 16; ++q) sw[8 + 16 * lane + q] = R[q];
#endif
            ++nb;
#if IH_CHAINS == 2
            if (chainb) {
                uint32_t* const sw2 = tb + nb * IH_TS;
#pragma unroll
#if IH_LANES16
                for (int q = 0; q < 8; ++q) sw2[8 + 16 * (lane >> 1) + 8 * (lane & 1u) + q] = R2[q];
#else
                for (int q = 0; q < 16; ++q) sw2[8 + 16 * lane + q] = R2[q];
#endif
                ++nb;
                gid += slots;   // the batch's second nonce is consumed by this iteration; the loop's own stride adds the next
            }
            const bool last = gid + slots >= count;   // the group's last nonce of the range (chain B's when it ran)
#else
            const bool last = gid + slots >= count;   // this group's last nonce of the range
#endif
            if (nb == IH_TUNE_TAIL || last) {
                __syncwarp(mask);
                if (lane < nb) {
                    // lane l hashes slot l: M over the 19 tag bytes || S || the 128 state words (563 bytes), then F
                    const uint32_t* const sr = tb + lane * IH_TS;
                    b3_words_xof<19u + 4u * 136u>(0, [&](uint32_t i) {
                        if (i < 4) return tm[i];
                        if (i == 4) return tm[4] | (sr[0] << 24);
                        const uint32_t j = i - 4;   // j in [1, 136]: source words 0 .. 135 are S then the state
                        const uint32_t prev = sr[j - 1], cur = j < 136u ? sr[j] : 0u;
                        return (prev >> 8) | (cur << 24);
                    }, o);
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
                    uint32_t Sl[8];
#pragma unroll
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) Sl[k] = sr[k];
                    uint32_t fm[33];
#pragma unroll
                    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) fm[i] = tf[i];
                    uint32_t prev = 0;
#pragma unroll
                    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < 28; ++j) {
                        const uint32_t cur = j < 11 ? pw[5 + j] : (j < 19 ? Sl[j - 11] : (j < 27 ? M[j - 19] : 0u));
                        fm[5 + j] = j == 0 ? (tf[5] | (cur << 8)) : ((prev >> 24) | (cur << 8));
                        prev = cur;
                    }
                    b3_words_xof<129>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return fm[i]; }, o);
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
                    bool le = true;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                        const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
                        const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
                        if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
                        if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
                    }
                    if (le && ih_live && IH_OCT_ONE) {
                        const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
                        if (rs < max_results) {
                            uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                            const uint64_t nl = start_nonce + (uint64_t)(gid0 + lane * slots);
                            e[0] = (uint32_t)nl; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nl >> 32);
                            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
                        }
                    }
                }
                __syncwarp(mask);
                nb = 0;
            }
        }
#elif IH_TUNE_WORDS == 1 || IH_TUNE_WORDS == 3 || IH_TUNE_WORDS == 4 || IH_TUNE_WORDS == 5  // M and F from words (3: this block alone; 4: M from words, F from bytes; 5: M from bytes, F from words)
        {
            // the message: 19 tag bytes || S (8 words) || the state words (16 per lane, lane-major) [|| the fold, 4 per lane];
            // source word j (S then state) sits at byte 19 + 4 j, so message word 4 + j = src[j-1] >> 8 | src[j] << 24.
            // Every lane computes the same hash in lockstep (the shuffles need every lane); lane 0 reports.
            const uint32_t nsrc = 8u + 128u + (IH_PAD ? 32u : 0u);
            const uint32_t mlen = 19u + 4u * nsrc;
            auto src = [&](uint32_t j) -> uint32_t {
                if (j >= nsrc) return 0u;
                if (j < 8) return S[j];
                const uint32_t q = j - 8;
                if (q < 128) return __shfl_sync(mask, R[q & 15], IH_SRC8(q >> 4), IH_W8);
#if IH_PAD
                return __shfl_sync(mask, f[(q - 128) & 3], IH_SRC8((q - 128) >> 2), IH_W8);
#else
                return 0u;
#endif
            };
#if IH_TUNE_WORDS == 5
            {   // bisect: M by the byte path on lane 0, broadcast to the group
                uint32_t mn = 0;
                if (lane == 0) {
                    mn = ih_tag(buf, 5);
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[mn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[mn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[mn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[mn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
                    mn += 32;
                }
                for (int l = 0; l < 8; ++l) for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
                    const uint32_t w = __shfl_sync(mask, R[k], IH_SRC8(l), IH_W8);
                    if (lane == 0) { const uint32_t pos = mn + 64 * l + 4 * k; buf[pos] = (uint8_t)w; buf[pos + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); buf[pos + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); buf[pos + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
                }
                if (lane == 0) { b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, mn + 512, 0, o); for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k]; }
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = __shfl_sync(mask, M[k], IH_SRC8(0), IH_W8);
            }
#else
            b3_words_xof<19u + 4u * (8u + 128u + (IH_PAD ? 32u : 0u))>(0, [&](uint32_t i) {
                if (i < 4) return tm[i];
                if (i == 4) return tm[4] | (src(0) << 24);
                const uint32_t j = i - 4;  // j >= 1
                return (src(j - 1) >> 8) | (src(j) << 24);
            }, o);
            for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
#endif
            // F = BLAKE3(tag(T_FINAL) || C || V || P || S || M): 21 tag bytes then 27 source words at byte 21 (word 5, byte 1)
            // C || V || P are prefix bytes 20..63 = words 5..15 of the seed prefix, then S, then M: 27 source words from byte 21
#if IH_TUNE_WORDS == 4
            {   // bisect: F by the byte path on lane 0, broadcast
                if (lane == 0) {
                    uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 44; ++i) buf[fn + i] = prefix[20 + i];
                    fn += 44;
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
                    fn += 32;
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
                    fn += 32;
                    b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
                    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
                }
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = __shfl_sync(mask, F[k], IH_SRC8(0), IH_W8);
            }
#else
            {   // the 33 message words built explicitly (the lambda form of this builder mismatched every vector on sm_120
                // while exact on sm_75 and sm_86, 10 Oct 2026; M's lambda builder is exact on all three)
                uint32_t fm[33];
#pragma unroll
                for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) fm[i] = tf[i];
                uint32_t prev = 0;
#pragma unroll
                for (uint32_t j = 0; j < 28; ++j) {
                    const uint32_t cur = j < 11 ? pw[5 + j] : (j < 19 ? S[j - 11] : (j < 27 ? M[j - 19] : 0u));
                    fm[5 + j] = j == 0 ? (tf[5] | (cur << 8)) : ((prev >> 24) | (cur << 8));
                    prev = cur;
                }
                b3_words_xof<129>(0, [&](uint32_t i) { return fm[i]; }, o);
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
            }
#endif
            if (lane == 0) {
                bool le = true;
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                    const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
                    const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
                    if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
                    if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
                }
                if (le && ih_live && IH_OCT_ONE) {
                    const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
                    if (rs < max_results) {
                        uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                        e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
                        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
                    }
                }
            }
        }
#else
        {
            uint32_t mn = 0;
            if (lane == 0) {
                mn = ih_tag(buf, 5);
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[mn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[mn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[mn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[mn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
                mn += 32;
            }
            for (int l = 0; l < 8; ++l) {
#pragma unroll
                for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
                    const uint32_t w = __shfl_sync(mask, R[k], IH_SRC8(l), IH_W8);
                    if (lane == 0) { const uint32_t pos = mn + 64 * l + 4 * k; buf[pos] = (uint8_t)w; buf[pos + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); buf[pos + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); buf[pos + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
                }
            }
#if IH_PAD
            for (int l = 0; l < 8; ++l) {
#pragma unroll
                for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
                    const uint32_t w = __shfl_sync(mask, f[j], IH_SRC8(l), IH_W8);
                    if (lane == 0) { const uint32_t pos = mn + 512 + 16 * l + 4 * j; buf[pos] = (uint8_t)w; buf[pos + 1] = (uint8_t)(w >> 8); buf[pos + 2] = (uint8_t)(w >> 16); buf[pos + 3] = (uint8_t)(w >> 24); }
                }
            }
#else
            (void)f;
#endif
            if (lane == 0) {
                b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, mn + 512 + (IH_PAD ? 128 : 0), 0, o);
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) M[k] = o[k];
                // F = BLAKE3(tag(T_FINAL) || C || V || P || S || M)
                uint32_t fn = ih_tag(buf, 6);
                for (int i = 0; i < 44; ++i) buf[fn + i] = prefix[20 + i];  // C || V || P from the seed prefix
                fn += 44;
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)S[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(S[k] >> 24); }
                fn += 32;
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) { buf[fn + 4 * k] = (uint8_t)M[k]; buf[fn + 4 * k + 1] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 8); buf[fn + 4 * k + 2] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 16); buf[fn + 4 * k + 3] = (uint8_t)(M[k] >> 24); }
                fn += 32;
                b3_chunk_xof(nullptr, buf, fn, 0, o);
                for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) F[k] = o[k];
                bool le = true;
                for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
                    const uint32_t av = bswap32(F[i]);
                    const uint32_t bv = params[8 + i];
                    if (av < bv) { le = true; break; }
                    if (av > bv) { le = false; break; }
                }
                if (le && ih_live && IH_OCT_ONE) {
                    const uint32_t rs = atomicAdd(&results[0], 1u);
                    if (rs < max_results) {
                        uint32_t* e = results + 1 + rs * RESULT_WORDS;
                        e[0] = (uint32_t)nonce; e[1] = (uint32_t)(nonce >> 32);
                        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[2 + i] = M[i];
                        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) e[10 + i] = F[i];
                    }
                }
            }
        }
#endif
        __syncwarp(mask);
    }
}
#endif  // IH_R16
